### [1] [SAGE: Semantic Attribute Graphs for Multi-Entity Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04255)
*Yongjoo Kim,Mincheol Kwon,Seonga Choi,Minseung Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Hyunyoung Lee,Yunsu Choi,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE框架解决密集文档图像中细粒度检索的"语义稀释"问题，通过将语义实体解析为分层图节点并用多向量嵌入表示，以迭代的子图匹配进行检索；同时引入DEAR数据集（1,055个查询-图像对）用于评测多实体视觉对比查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视觉语言检索器将裁剪区域编码为单一向量，在密集文档图像中容易混合多个不同实体的信号，导致细粒度检索所需的证据被掩盖，作者将其定义为"语义稀释"（Semantic Dilution）失败模式。

Method: SAGE是一个免训练（training-free）框架，包含三个步骤：1）从密集文档图像中解析语义实体；2）将实体表示为带有多向量嵌入的分层图节点；3）通过迭代的实体级子图匹配检索与查询相关的证据。同时构建DEAR数据集（1,055个查询-图像对），来自产品详情页，涵盖四种递增复杂度的查询类型。

Result: SAGE显著减少语义稀释现象，在DEAR上优于基于patch级和基于OCR的检索基线，多实体视觉对比查询上达到Recall@3为0.849，生成分数为2.746。

Conclusion: 通过实体级多向量表示和子图匹配，SAGE有效解决了密集文档图像中单向量编码导致的语义稀释问题，为细粒度跨模态检索提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Dense document images often contain many fine-grained visual and textual entities whose relevance depends on a user query. Standard vision-language retrievers encode cropped regions with a single vector, which can mix distinct entity signals and obscure the evidence needed for fine-grained retrieval. We call this failure mode Semantic Dilution and quantitatively show that it degrades entity-level retrieval as a function of entity density. To mitigate it, we propose SAGE, a training-free framework that parses semantic entities from dense document images, represents them as hierarchical graph nodes with multi-vector embeddings, and retrieves query-relevant evidence through iterative entity-level subgraph matching. We also introduce DEAR, a dataset of 1,055 query--image pairs sourced from product detail pages, where each query requires retrieving and comparing multiple fine-grained entities from visually dense inputs across four question types of increasing complexity. Experiments show that SAGE substantially reduces semantic dilution and outperforms patch-level and OCR-based retrieval baselines on DEAR, achieving a Recall@3 of 0.849 and a generation score of 2.746 on multi-entity visual comparison queries. Our code is available at https://github.com/All4Nothing/SAGE.

</details>


### [2] [MURAL: Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04574)
*Ahmad Mousavi,Majid Alikhani,Yeon-Chang Lee,Roberto Corizzo,Yeganeh Abdollahinejad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MURAL是一个多模态图神经推荐框架，通过自适应边学习（Adaptive Edge Learner）和不确定性感知融合（Uncertainty-Aware Fusion）解决了现有方法中的结构刚性和语义脆弱性问题，在TikTok和Amazon基准上显著超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图神经网络推荐方法面临两大瓶颈：(1) 结构刚性——依赖静态预计算相似度图，无法适应动态演变的用户偏好；(2) 语义脆弱——噪声模态信号被无差别融合，扭曲了协同过滤信号。

Method: 提出MURAL框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Adaptive Edge Learner：结合可微检索增强策略与近似最近邻搜索（O(NlogN)复杂度），动态发现潜在物品-物品相关性；(2) Uncertainty-Aware Fusion：建模异质模态的aleatoric不确定性，动态降低不可靠特征权重并优先高置信度信号；(3) 对比式师生对齐：将模态特定表示锚定到稳定的行为信号上，确保优化稳定性且无梯度泄露。

Result: 在大规模基准（TikTok和Amazon）上的实验表明，MURAL显著超越结构性和生成式SOTA基线，在精度上取得更优表现，同时提供基于领域特定模态优势的可解释性，并在极端数据损坏情况下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MURAL证明了将多模态推荐从固定的结构增强转向动态拓扑发现的有效性，通过自适应边的学习和不确定性驱动的融合机制，在动态偏好场景中实现了更加鲁棒、可解释且精准的推荐性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Graph Neural Networks have become standard for recommendation by augmenting sparse interaction data with content features. Yet current architectures face two bottlenecks: structural rigidity, from a reliance on static precomputed similarity graphs that cannot adapt to evolving preferences; and semantic fragility, where noisy modality signals are indiscriminately fused, distorting the collaborative signal. We propose MURAL (Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning), a unified framework that shifts multimodal recommendation from fixed structural augmentation to dynamic topology discovery. To address structural rigidity, an Adaptive Edge Learner combines a differentiable retrieval-augmented strategy with an approximate nearest neighbor search to discover latent item-item correlations that are both semantically adaptive and computationally scalable (O(NlogN)). To address semantic fragility, an Uncertainty-Aware Fusion module models the aleatoric uncertainty of heterogeneous modalities, dynamically down-weighting unreliable features while prioritizing high-confidence signals as a defense against cross-modal noise. We further employ a contrastive teacher-student alignment that anchors modality-specific representations to stable behavioral signals, ensuring optimization stability without gradient leakage. Experiments on large-scale benchmarks including TikTok and Amazon show that MURAL significantly surpasses both structural and generative state-of-the-art baselines, achieving superior accuracy while offering interpretability through domain-specific modality dominance and robustness under extreme data corruption.

</details>


### [3] [Latent-Aligned Reasoning for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04645)
*Jiarui Jin,Anyang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LARK（Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK），一个两阶段潜在推理框架，通过可学习潜在令牌与链式推理交错并结合互补对齐机制，解决多模态视觉-语言模型在推荐任务中跨模态信号随多步推理逐层衰减（cross-modal dilution）的问题。在三个公开基准和一个工业数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态视觉-语言模型（VLM）在跨模态理解中表现出色，但应用于推荐时存在根本性挑战：随着多步推理的进行，视觉和文本信号逐步衰减，作者将此现象称为"跨模态稀释"（cross-modal dilution），这会损害最终推荐表征的质量。

Method: LARK采用两阶段潜在推理框架，在单一VLM内实现互补对齐机制。第一阶段：将可学习的潜在令牌与多步链式思维（CoT）推理交错，并显式与冻结的视觉编码器对齐，作为视觉检查点在推理链中保持感知细节。第二阶段：通过桥接MLP投影潜在表示，用物品间对比学习训练；为防止推理语义衰减，将中间特征与第一阶段的CoT隐藏状态对齐，将最终嵌入锚定到模型自身的推理输出上。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集和一个工业数据集上的实验表明，LARK在多种推荐架构下均达到最先进的性能，受控消融实验确认了每个组件的独立贡献。

Conclusion: LARK通过潜在令牌与CoT推理交错以及两阶段的互补对齐机制，有效缓解了多模态推荐中跨模态稀释问题，验证了利用VLM推理能力增强推荐系统的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in cross-modal understanding, yet a fundamental challenge persists when applying them to recommendation: as representations propagate through multi-step reasoning, both visual and textual signals progressively attenuate - a phenomenon we term cross-modal dilution. To address this, we propose LARK (Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK), a two-stage latent reasoning framework with complementary alignment mechanisms within a single VLM. In the first stage, learnable latent tokens are interleaved with multi-step chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and explicitly aligned with a frozen vision encoder, serving as visual checkpoints that preserve perceptual details throughout the reasoning chain. In the second stage, the latent representations are projected via a bridge MLP and trained with item-to-item contrastive learning; to prevent the reasoning semantics from fading, intermediate features are aligned with the CoT hidden states from the first stage, anchoring the final embeddings to the model's own reasoning output. Experiments on three public benchmarks and one industrial dataset show that LARK achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple recommendation architectures, with controlled ablations confirming the distinct contribution of each component.

</details>


### [4] [VizIt: A multi-view framework for exploring single-cell, spatial, and genetic data online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04658)
*Chenhang Christopher Zhang,Yanqing Lou,Jie Yuan,Mingming Lu,Jacob Parker,Himanshu Chintalapudi,Zechuan Lin,Clemens R. Scherzer,Yuxuan Hu,Ruifeng Hu,Xianjun Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VizIt是一个开源框架，用于单细胞和空间转录组、表观基因组及遗传数据的多视图交互式探索，通过连接基因、细胞类型、条件、空间、基因组区域和变异为中心的视图，实现跨生物视角的无缝导航，并以帕金森细胞图谱作为应用演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学研究中需要从互补的生物学视角审视数据，但现有的交互式探索在不同模态和工具之间仍然碎片化，缺乏统一的探索框架。

Method: VizIt通过将基因、细胞类型、条件（condition）、空间、基因组区域和变异等不同生物学视角的视图进行连接，构建多视图探索框架，支持单细胞和空间转录组、表观基因组和遗传数据的综合分析。

Result: 以帕金森细胞图谱（Parkinson's Cell Atlas）为例，展示了VizIt作为可定制的交互式多组学资源的能力。

Conclusion: VizIt提供了一个统一的多视图探索框架，弥合了多组学数据在不同生物学视角之间的交互式分析鸿沟。

Abstract: Multi-omic studies increasingly require data to be examined from complementary biological perspectives, yet interactive exploration remains fragmented across modalities and tools. We present VizIt, an open-source framework for multi-view exploration of single-cell and spatial transcriptomic, epigenomic and genetic data. VizIt connects gene-, cell type-, condition-, spatial-, genomic region- and variant-centered views, enabling seamless navigation across biological perspectives. We demonstrate VizIt through the Parkinson's Cell Atlas, a customizable interactive multi-omic resource.

</details>


### [5] [Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization for Training-Free AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04813)
*Wei Zhou,Tiandeng Wu,Jiandong Ding,Zhufeng Fan,Yi Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IGPO是一种面向AI搜索系统的免训练优化方法，将策略与环境事实分离，通过在线探测商品库存并构建库存画像来指导检索和选择提示，离线通过随机游走分组对比信号优化策略指南，在商业智能助手搜索系统中实现3.17%的相对CTR提升和38.9%不良案例减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI搜索系统早期部署通常面对频繁更新的商品目录，商品及其属性不能视为可在固定提示或策略中编码的稳定知识。微调、强化学习和静态提示补丁均不适合：标签稀缺、奖励随库存漂移、模型发布成本高且提示修复快速失效。

Method: IGPO将策略与环境事实解耦：离线学习针对运行时库存证据而非记忆可用商品的策略指南（Policy Guidelines）；在线时通过探测库存构建库存画像，将相关策略指南注入检索和选择提示。离线阶段按查询分组进行随机游走，混合结果组直接提供对比信号，库存引导的探索循环区分"遗漏检索路径"与"库存证据下无匹配支持"两种情况。

Result: 自2026年5月起部署于商业智能助手AI搜索系统。14天在线A/B测试显示完整IGPO方案带来3.17%的相对CTR提升和38.9%的可审计不良案例减少。

Conclusion: IGPO证明在动态商品目录场景下，无需重新训练模型即可提升固定AI搜索管线的策略质量，通过策略与环境事实的分离设计，实现对库存漂移的鲁棒适应并有效降低不良案例。

Abstract: Early in deployment, an AI search system typically operates over a frequently updated product catalog, so the available items and their properties cannot be treated as stable knowledge that can be encoded in fixed prompts or strategies. Fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and static prompt patches fit poorly: labels are scarce, rewards drift with inventory, model releases are costly, and prompt fixes quickly stale. We present Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization (IGPO), a training-free approach for fixed AI search pipelines. IGPO separates policy from environment facts: it learns Policy Guidelines for acting on runtime inventory evidence rather than memorizing available items. Online, IGPO grounds each query by probing the inventory and constructing an inventory portrait, then injects relevant Policy Guidelines into the retrieval and selection prompts. Offline, stochastic rollouts are grouped by query -- mixed outcome groups directly yield contrastive signal, and an inventory-guided exploration loop distinguishes missed retrieval routes from cases where no matching support is found under the observed inventory evidence. Since May 2026, IGPO has been deployed in a commercial smart-assistant AI search system. A 14-day online A/B test of the complete IGPO treatment shows a 3.17% relative CTR lift and a 38.9% reduction in audited bad cases.

</details>


### [6] [Personalized Task Dependency Graphs for Mitigating Signal Erosion in Multi-Task Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04862)
*Fuyuan Liu,Tiandeng Wu,Yaqun Fang,Wei Zhou,Zehao Zhou,Wenping Chen,Qishun Mei,Jiaxin Zhou,Heng Chang,Yi Cao,Jiandong Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出个性化任务依赖图（PTDG）方法，通过低秩近似动态调整商品间任务依赖强度，并结合GCN传播和自适应渐进掩码策略，解决工业推荐中多任务学习因固定漏斗结构导致的信号衰减问题。在KuaiRand1K和工业数据集上，稀疏转化任务AUC提升最高1.45%，线上A/B测试CVR提升1.2%、eCPM提升1.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTL方法在静态转化漏斗上施加均匀依赖强度，忽略了任务相关性随商品特性自然变化的事实；沿固定链路的层级消息传递会导致累积信号衰减，恶化稀疏深层漏斗目标的表现。作者希望打破这种刚性架构限制，让任务依赖关系根据商品特征自适应调整。

Method: 1）提出个性化任务依赖图（PTDG），在保留物理因果约束（如点击→支付）的前提下，通过低秩近似为每个商品动态重连/调整任务依赖路径的强度以增强结构鲁棒性；2）采用带硬因果掩码的GCN传播来建立自适应信息捷径；3）提出自适应渐进掩码（APM）策略，根据任务稀疏度解耦共享参数，稳定优化过程。

Result: 在KuaiRand1K和工业数据集上，稀疏转化任务AUC最高提升1.45%，同时稠密目标性能基本持平；线上A/B测试中CVR相对基线提升1.2%，eCPM提升1.9%。

Conclusion: PTDG通过个性化任务依赖建模和硬因果约束下的自适应信息传播，有效缓解了固定漏斗结构带来的信号衰减问题，在稀疏深层转化目标上取得了显著且稳定的提升，验证了任务相关性随商品特性动态变化的假设。

Abstract: Optimizing multiple conversion objectives is a core challenge in industrial recommendation, often limited by signal erosion in rigid architectures. Existing Multi-Task Learning (MTL) methods typically enforce uniform dependency strengths across a static conversion funnel, overlooking how task correlations naturally vary based on item characteristics. Hierarchical message passing along these fixed chains leads to cumulative signal attenuation, which degrades performance on sparse, deep-funnel objectives. To address this, we propose the Personalized Task Dependency Graphs (PTDG). While respecting necessary physical causal constraints (e.g., Click -> Pay), PTDG dynamically "rewires" the intensity of dependency pathways for each item via low-rank approximation to ensure structural robustness. We implement a GCN-based propagation with hard causal masking to establish adaptive information shortcuts. Additionally, we introduce an Adaptive Progressive Masking (APM) strategy that decouples shared parameters according to task sparsity, helping to stabilize optimization. Experiments on KuaiRand1K and an industrial dataset show that PTDG significantly improves AUC on sparse conversion tasks by up to 1.45%, while maintaining comparable performance on dense objectives. Online A/B testing shows PTDG improves Conversion Rate (CVR) by 1.2% and effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM) by 1.9% relative to the baseline.

</details>


### [7] [AtomRec: Evolving Atomic Memory for Agentic Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04882)
*Peiyu Hu,Weihai Lu,Siying Gu,Zhuodong Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Yuean Niu,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AtomRec，一个具有演化原子协同记忆的智能体推荐系统。它将用户和物品记忆表示为结构化原子单元，构建语义链接，并在新交互到来时演化相关历史字段。推荐时检索链接记忆作为多跳证据路径，使协同信号支持有依据的排序。在四个公共基准上平均相对提升约8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的智能体推荐系统中的记忆机制通常将用户和物品信息压缩为粗粒度的摘要，并以标量协同链接连接，难以保留细粒度的偏好阶段或在用户兴趣演化时检索可解释的证据。

Method: AtomRec包含三个核心设计：(1) 将用户和物品记忆表示为结构化原子单元；(2) 在相关记忆之间构建语义链接；(3) 新交互到来时演化相关历史字段。推荐阶段检索链接记忆作为多跳证据路径，而非孤立的邻居摘要。

Result: 在四个公共基准上，AtomRec一致优于最先进的智能体和记忆增强基线，各指标平均相对提升约8.5%。

Conclusion: AtomRec通过原子化记忆表示与多跳证据路径检索，有效解决了传统记忆压缩导致的信息丢失和证据不可解释问题，展示了演化协同记忆在智能体推荐中的优越性。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems use large language models to maintain semantic memory and support evidence-aware recommendation. However, existing memory mechanisms often compress user and item information into coarse summaries and connect them with scalar collaborative links, making it difficult to preserve fine-grained preference stages or retrieve interpretable evidence as user interests evolve. We propose \textsc{AtomRec}, an agentic recommender with evolving atomic collaborative memory. \textsc{AtomRec} represents user and item memories as structured atomic units, builds semantic links across related memories, and evolves related historical fields when new interactions arrive. During recommendation, it retrieves linked memories as multi-hop evidence paths rather than isolated neighbor summaries, allowing collaborative signals to support grounded ranking. Experiments on four public benchmarks show that \textsc{AtomRec} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art agentic and memory-augmented baselines, with around 8.5\% average relative improvement across metrics.

</details>


### [8] [SAM-D2Q: Aligning Multimodal Doc2Query with Search Demand and Conversion for E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04961)
*Hui Zhou,Jian Hui Ji,Lei Ma,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SAM-D2Q，一种面向电商搜索的、与业务目标对齐的多模态Doc2Query文档扩展框架，通过三阶段（多模态监督微调、多模态数据增强、基于强化学习的偏好对齐）生成商业有效的伪查询，在AliExpress线上系统中将GMV提升+3.38%、Pay Count提升+2.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索中用户查询与商家商品标题之间存在词汇不匹配问题。传统Doc2Query方法仅基于文本，未针对电商业务目标优化，生成的扩展可能语义合理但商业效果不佳，且会遗漏商品图像中的关键视觉属性。

Method: SAM-D2Q包含三个阶段：(1)任务适配的多模态监督微调，增强对商品标题、图像和用户查询的视觉-语言理解；(2)多模态数据增强，提升对关键视觉属性的感知和扩展覆盖；(3)基于强化学习的偏好对齐，引导模型生成更符合用户意图和商业价值的伪查询，在布尔检索约束下工作。

Result: 离线实验表明SAM-D2Q相较传统Doc2Query方法显著提升检索性能；已在AliExpress生产搜索系统中部署，线上业务指标GMV提升+3.38%，Pay Count提升+2.27%。

Conclusion: 将多模态信息引入文档扩展并结合业务导向的偏好对齐，能有效解决电商搜索中的词汇不匹配问题并驱动商业指标增长，验证了面向业务目标优化文档扩展的可行性。

Abstract: E-commerce search often suffers from vocabulary mismatch between user queries and merchant-authored product titles, since short titles cannot fully cover diverse user expressions or visual product attributes. Although Doc2Query alleviates this issue by generating pseudo-queries for document expansion, traditional methods are text-only and not optimized for e-commerce business objectives. As a result, they may produce semantically plausible but commercially ineffective expansions and miss key attributes present in product images. To this end, we propose E-commerce Search-Aligned Multimodal Doc2Query (SAM-D2Q), a business-aligned multimodal document expansion framework for e-commerce search under Boolean retrieval constraints. SAM-D2Q consists of three stages: (1) task-adapted multimodal supervised fine-tuning to enhance vision-language understanding of product titles, images, and user queries; (2) multimodal data augmentation to improve perception of key visual attributes and expansion coverage; and (3) reinforcement-learning-based preference alignment toward search business objectives, encouraging the model to generate pseudo-queries that better match user intent and commercial value. Offline experiments show that SAM-D2Q substantially improves retrieval performance over traditional Doc2Query methods. Deployed in the AliExpress production search system, SAM-D2Q improves online business metrics, increasing GMV by +3.38% and Pay Count by +2.27%.

</details>

*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探究特定重复品牌询问是否会在语言模型推荐中耗尽可能答案。通过300个问题-引擎测试单元（50个问题×6个引擎×15次运行，开放式提取1470个组织），发现无联网检索的5个引擎在第15次运行时仍有86-92%的单元出现从未见过的品牌；唯一开启网络检索的引擎在第10次运行后接近饱和（中位数8个组织，64%的单元仍新增）。引用域累积在所有测试时窗内持续上升。单次运行仅覆盖五次运行品牌集的62-77%。采用精确稀疏化与Chao2丰富度估计，并通过固定花名册提取对照证明花名册约束会人为制造平台期，而开放式提取能消除这一假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型在重复询问相同品牌问题时，其推荐是否会耗尽所有可能答案，以及是否耗尽取决于检索机制的启用与否。作者还指出以往固定花名册的提取方式会虚假地制造平台期。

Method: 设计300个问题-引擎组合单元（50个问题、6个引擎、每种运行15次），对1470个经裁定的组织进行开放式提取。采用精确稀疏化（exact rarefaction）与Chao2丰富度估计作为指标；设置对照实验，用固定花名册提取在相同回答上重现平坦曲线，以验证提取方式的影响。

Result: 无检索引擎第15次运行仍持续新增品牌（86-92%的单元）；检索引擎列表在中位数8个组织处收敛且64%的单元仍在新增；深层运行在运行24时仍增添引用域，Chao2下界估计仅观察到59-84%；单次运行仅见五次品牌集的62-77%；不同引擎的答案集差异明显，中位数15个组织仅在一个引擎中出现。

Conclusion: 重复问题是否会耗尽品牌推荐，根本上取决于检索：无检索时的开放式输出几乎不收敛，而启用网络检索可显著加速饱和。方法层面的关键结论是：固定花名册（roster-bounded）追踪会制造人为的平台期，开放式提取才反映真实动态。

Abstract: Whether repeated identical buying questions exhaust a language model's brand recommendations depends on retrieval. Across 300 question-engine cells (50 questions, six engines, 15 runs each, open extraction over 1,470 adjudicated organizations), the five engines answering without web search were still adding never-seen brands at run 15 in 86-92% of cells, with median repertoires of 15-31 organizations; the one retrieval-enabled engine closed its list (median 8 organizations, 64% of cells still adding), matching four earlier deep cells where web-search runs saturated by run ten. Cited-domain accumulation keeps rising at every horizon tested: four deep cells were still adding domains at run 24 with 59-84% of the Chao2 lower-bound estimate observed, and 44% of the retrieval engine's breadth cells were still adding domains at run 15. A single run shows 62-77% of the five-run brand set, and across engines the median question draws 38 organizations, of which a median of 15 appear in exactly one engine. Estimators are exact rarefaction and Chao2 richness; a parallel fixed-roster extraction reproduces flat curves on identical responses, so roster-bounded tracking manufactures plateaus that open extraction removes.

</details>

*Aleksandra Osowska-Kurczab,Klaudia Nazarko,Eliška Kosturová,Lidia Wojciechowska,Michał Bień*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlleCompanion，一个部署于Allegro.com的生产级互补产品推荐检索框架。该框架通过数据级过滤启发式与类别约束的双塔架构相结合，并引入ComCat（多源互补类别映射层），将大规模共购行为信号转化为精确的语义兼容性推荐，显著提升了GMV和收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推荐模型无法区分"仅仅被一起购买"的商品与"真正功能互补"的商品，导致互补产品推荐效果不佳。例如，购买专业相机时系统应推荐匹配的镜头而非通用三脚架或另一台相机机身。大规模共购流量中固有的噪声信号使得建模真实用户行为极具挑战性。

Method: 提出AlleCompanion检索框架，核心方法包括：(1) 数据级过滤启发式，消除大规模共购流量中的固有噪声；(2) 类别约束的双塔（Two Tower）架构，其中的Category Adapter在嵌入空间引导模型，将候选集限制在逻辑互补的边界内；(3) 引入ComCat多源互补类别映射，作为翻译层将嘈杂流量中的有意义模式蒸馏为可维护、可控制的解决方案，整合专家规则、人在环路反馈、LLM推理和统计挖掘。

Result: 实验表明，将显式类别级约束与神经架构结合能有效过滤共购噪声，提供满足真实用户需求的推荐。该框架每月服务超过2000万活跃用户，为有机发现带来了显著的GMV提升，并在赞助广告位中推动了可观的收入增长。

Conclusion: 显式的类别级约束与神经架构的有机结合是过滤共购噪声、实现精准互补产品推荐的有效途径，该方案已在生产环境中验证了其商业价值。

Abstract: When a customer adds a professional camera to their cart, should the system suggest a matching lens, a generic tripod, or another camera body? Complementary Product Recommendation is vital for comprehensive basket building, yet standard models often fail to distinguish between items that are merely bought together and those that truly work together. In this paper, we present AlleCompanion: a production-scale retrieval framework deployed at Allegro.com that transforms noisy behavioural signals into precise semantic compatibility. We mitigate the intrinsic noise in large-scale co-purchase traffic by combining data-level filtering heuristics with a category-constrained Two Tower architecture. Within this framework, the Category Adapter guides the model in the embedding space, constraining candidates within logically complementary boundaries. Since modelling authentic user behaviour at scale is inherently difficult, we introduce ComCat, a multi-source Complementary Categories Mapping. ComCat acts as a translational layer that distils meaningful patterns from noisy traffic into a maintainable and controllable solution, integrating expert rules, human-in-the-loop feedback, LLM-based reasoning, and statistical mining. Our experimental results demonstrate that combining explicit category-level constraints with neural architectures effectively filters out co-purchase noise to surface recommendations that satisfy real-world user needs. Serving over 20 million active users monthly, the framework delivers significant uplifts in attributed GMV for organic discovery and drives substantial revenue growth in sponsored placements.

</details>


### [11] [Embedding Surgery: Localized Updates for Adaptive Ranking Correction in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05110)
*Maddalena Amendola,Antonio Mallia,Raffaele Perego*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"embedding surgery"（嵌入手术）方法，通过查询时对密集检索系统中选定的文档嵌入进行局部最小化更新来纠正排名，以适应反馈和演化中的搜索意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索系统是现代搜索引擎、推荐平台和检索增强生成管道的核心组件。由于文档嵌入是离线计算并存储在静态索引中的，这些系统难以适应编辑反馈、用户交互或演化中的搜索意图。这种静态性限制了系统的自适应能力。

Method: 将嵌入手术形式化为一个凸优化问题：在保持排名约束的同时最小化对受影响文档表示的修改。在查询时对选定的文档嵌入进行局部化最小更新，引导信号来自编辑反馈、用户交互或大语言模型生成的伪标签。集成到标准密集检索管道中，通过简单的原地覆盖操作即可应用于可扩展的近似最近邻索引。

Result: 在TREC Deep Learning、TREC Robust、TREC CAsT和MS MARCO基准上进行评估。结果显示一致改进，例如在DL-Hard上编辑反馈下nDCG@10相对提升高达+60.64%。即使反馈带噪或漂移，仍表现良好，计算成本低且不破坏嵌入空间的全局结构。排名纠正可传播到语义相关的查询，与Rocchio等查询适应方法互补。

Conclusion: 嵌入手术是一种轻量、高效、可扩展的排名纠错方法，能够在不重建索引的情况下让静态密集检索系统适应动态反馈，且与现有的查询适应方法互补，对噪声反馈更鲁棒。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems are core components of modern search engines, recommendation platforms, and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines. They encode documents and queries into dense embeddings, enabling efficient semantic search via vector similarity. However, because document embeddings are computed offline and stored in static indexes, these systems struggle to adapt to user feedback or evolving search intent. To address this limitation, we introduce \emph{embedding surgery}, a lightweight approach for adaptive ranking correction in dense retrieval. The method applies localized, minimal updates to selected document embeddings at query time, guided by editorial feedback, user interactions, or pseudo-labels from large language models. We formulate embedding surgery as a convex optimization problem that enforces ranking constraints while minimizing modifications to the affected document representations. We integrate embedding surgery into standard dense retrieval pipelines and evaluate it on TREC Deep Learning, TREC Robust, TREC CAsT, and MS MARCO benchmarks. Results show consistent improvements (e.g., up to +60.64\% relative improvement in nDCG@10 on DL-Hard under editorial feedback), even under noisy or shifting feedback, with low computational cost and without disrupting the global structure of the embedding space. Extensive experiments show that ranking corrections propagate to semantically related queries and that embedding updates can be applied safely and efficiently to scalable Approximate Nearest Neighbor indexes via simple in-place overwriting, without requiring costly index reconstruction. Finally, embedding surgery complements query adaptation methods such as CoRocchio, yielding additional gains while being more robust to noisy feedback.

</details>

### [12] [DTM: Deterministic Approaches for Black-box Test Suite Minimization with Tree-based Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04205)
*Md Siam,Shartaz Sajid Nahid,Md Arif Hasan,Nurul Ahad Tawhid,Kazi Sakib*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DTM框架，通过将测试用例转化为抽象语法树并利用四种树形相似度度量，结合三种确定性子集选择算法（修正最小生成树、谱聚类、动态规划），实现黑盒测试套件最小化的完全确定性。在Defects4J的16个Java项目、661个缺陷版本上评估，平均准确率0.74，执行时间仅0.98分钟，且多次运行结果完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效的黑盒测试套件最小化技术依赖进化搜索算法，产生非确定性，导致多次运行结果不一致，削弱了自动化测试流水线的可靠性。需要一个既能保持效率和有效性，又能保证确定性的测试套件缩减方法。

Method: DTM框架包含两个阶段：首先将测试用例转换为抽象语法树（AST），使用四种基于树的相似度度量计算测试用例间的两两相似性；然后采用三种确定性算法（修正最小生成树、谱聚类、动态规划）进行子集选择，以缩减测试套件。

Result: 在16个Java项目（来自Defects4J，共661个缺陷版本）上评估，DTM达到平均准确率0.74，执行时间仅0.98分钟，优于所有现有最优方法，并且多次运行产生完全相同的结果，保证了完全的确定性。

Conclusion: DTM成功解决了黑盒测试套件最小化中的非确定性问题，在保持乃至超越现有方法有效性和效率的同时，为自动化测试流水线提供了可靠且可复现的测试缩减方案。

Abstract: Black-box Test Suite Minimization (TSM) techniques reduce testing costs without requiring access to production code. However, existing effective approaches rely on evolutionary search algorithms, introducing non-determinism that produces inconsistent results across runs, undermining reliability in automated testing pipelines. We propose DTM (Deterministic approaches for black-box Test suite Minimization), a framework that ensures deterministic test suite reduction while preserving effectiveness and efficiency. DTM converts test cases into Abstract Syntax Trees and computes pairwise similarities using four tree-based measures. For subset selection, it employs three deterministic algorithms: Modified Minimum Spanning Tree, Spectral Clustering, and Dynamic Programming. We evaluated DTM on 16 Java projects from Defects4J with 661 buggy versions. Experimental results show that DTM achieved an average accuracy of 0.74 with an execution time of just 0.98 minutes, outperforming all state-of-the-art approaches. Moreover, it consistently produced identical results across multiple runs, ensuring full determinism.

</details>


### [13] [Breaking the Alphabet: Rethinking File Ordering in Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04207)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Zadia Codabux,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模调查（1,355名开发者、182个开源项目）研究了PR中文件排序对代码评审的影响，发现默认字母排序与人类认知不匹配：仅10.2%的评审者认为字母排序最优，超过半数报告其增加上下文切换和评审疲劳，63.9%担心会遗漏bug，66%的评审者希望引入依赖感知分组和可自定义排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代码评审对软件质量至关重要，但现有研究很少探讨PR中变更文件的排序顺序如何影响评审有效性。主流代码评审工具默认按字母排序，注重可预测性而非上下文相关性，这种排序可能造成认知负担但缺乏实证证据。

Method: 采用混合方法（mixed-methods）进行首次大规模调查，涵盖1,355名专业开发者，来自182个广泛使用的开源项目，研究文件排序对评审行为、理解和感知有效性的影响。

Result: 仅10.2%的评审者认为字母排序最优；57.6%报告排序增加了上下文切换、干扰逻辑推理并导致评审疲劳；63.9%担心默认排序可能导致遗漏bug；66%的评审者期望依赖感知分组和可自定义文件排序。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了默认文件排序与人类认知之间存在系统性错位，凸显了设计以评审者为中心的界面（reviewer-centric）的必要性，使工具行为更好地与人类认知保持一致，例如引入依赖感知分组等改进。

Abstract: Effective code review is central to maintaining software quality, yet there is limited research about how the ordering of changed files in Pull Requests (PRs) influences review effectiveness. Most popular code review tools default to alphabetical ordering, favoring predictability over contextual relevance. While prior studies examined how file position shapes reviewer attention, it remains unclear how such ordering influences cognitive load and perceived review thoroughness. This study presents the first large-scale survey of 1,355 professional developers across 182 widely used open-source projects to investigate how file ordering impacts review behavior, comprehension, and perceived effectiveness. Our mixed-methods analysis reveals that only 10.2% of reviewers consider alphabetical ordering optimal, underscoring a cognitive misalignment in their interpretation of code changes. Although some developers appreciate its predictability, more than half (57.6%) report that it increases context switching, disrupts logical reasoning, and contributes to review fatigue, and 63.9% expressed concern that the default ordering may cause them to miss bugs. We further identify key challenges in multi-file reviews and elicit developers' expectations for improved tooling, including dependency-aware grouping and customizable file ordering (requested by 66% of reviewers). These findings highlight the need for reviewer-centric interface designs that better align tool behavior with human cognition.

</details>


### [14] [AI Writes Code, Humans Pay the Debt. An Empirical Study on the Sustainability and Evolution of Agent-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04208)
*Antonino Coppola,Matteo Esposito,Rick Kazman,Valentina Lenarduzzi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项大规模软件仓库挖掘研究，旨在系统调查生成式AI编码代理生成的代码对软件质量的影响，重点关注问题定位准确性、技术债务引入及其长期演变趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI编码代理在软件工程中被广泛采用，带来了短期生产力提升，但其对软件质量和长期技术债务的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统性的实证研究。

Method: 基于SQuaD数据集开展大规模MSR（挖掘软件仓库）研究，使用628k候选issue问题集，通过多个LLM代理为这些问题生成实现，并与开发者的实际提交进行对比分析。采用静态分析指标与工具，在提交级别和版本发布级别评估差异，并通过系统化基准测试策略选择多个LLM代理。

Result: 预期提供代理驱动开发引入权衡的实证证据，包括问题定位精度差异、技术债务引入变化、长期演化潜在分歧，并揭示不同LLM之间的性能差异。

Conclusion: 研究预期将丰富对软件演化和编码代理结合的理解，推动更负责任地采用Agent进行软件开发的实践决策。

Abstract: Context. The increasing adoption of Generative AI coding agents in software engineering is transforming how developers implement and maintain code. While these systems provide short-term productivity benefits, their long-term impact on software quality and technical debt remains unclear. Aim. We aim to investigate how agent-generated code affects software quality, focusing on issue localization accuracy, the introduction of technical debt, and its evolution over time. Method. We will conduct a large-scale mining software repositories study using the SQuaD dataset, employing a candidate set of 628k issue tickets. We will generate agent-based implementations for these issues, and compare them with the actual commits done by developers using static analysis metrics and tools. We will analyze differences at the commit level and across releases, and we will consider multiple LLM-based Agents selected through a systematic benchmarking strategy. Expected Results. We expect to provide empirical evidence on the trade-offs introduced by agent-based development, including differences in localization accuracy, variations in technical debt introduction, and potential divergence in long-term evolution. We expect the results to highlight variability across LLMs, to enrich our understanding of software evolution with Agents, and to inform more responsible adoption of Agents in software development.

</details>


### [15] [The Prompt Triangle: A Registered Report on Prompts as Hybrid Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04209)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Jan-Philipp Steghöfer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一份注册报告（Stage 1协议），提出了一个验证性研究方案，旨在探讨AI编码辅助工具中提示词（prompt）如何作为软件工程工件演化，以及它们与需求工程（RE）活动的一致性如何预测开发结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI编码助手正改变软件开发模式，从编写代码转向编写提示词来引导代码生成，但关于提示词如何作为软件工程工件运作的实证证据仍然匮乏。

Method: 基于Prompt Triangle概念模型（从功能性质量、通用解决方案、具体解决方案三个维度描述提示词），预注册了四个假设，分别考察提示词演化、开发者特征、RE活动一致性及时间模式。采用受控实验（n=30）、社区上传数据和挖掘数据，通过双重编码来检验提示模式是否预测开发成功。

Result: 本文报告的是Stage 1研究协议而非实证结果，预期产出包括对提示词演化规律的描述性结果，以及提示模式与开发成功之间关系的验证性证据。

Conclusion: 该研究为提示词作为软件工程工件提供了确认性实证框架，填补了AI辅助开发中提示工程与需求工程交叉领域的空白。

Abstract: AI-based coding assistants are transforming software development by shifting effort from writing code to crafting prompts that guide code generation. Despite their growing importance, little empirical evidence exists on how prompts function as software engineering artifacts. Building on prior work framing prompts as mixed artifacts combining requirement intent and solution guidance, we build on the Prompt Triangle, a conceptual model describing prompts along three components: Functionality and Quality (requirements), General Solutions (architectural guidance), and Specific Solutions (implementation constraints). This registered report presents the Stage 1 protocol for a confirmatory study investigating how prompts evolve and how their alignment with requirements engineering (RE) activities predicts development outcomes. We preregister four hypotheses examining prompt evolution, developer characteristics, RE activity alignment, and temporal patterns. We employ a controlled experiment (n=30), community uploads, and mined data using dual-coding to test whether prompting patterns predict development success.

</details>


### [16] [SH-PDOPS: AI-Driven Cloud Native Enterprise Reliability Framework for Predictive Analytics and Intelligent DevOps Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04210)
*Ayushman Bosu Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个AI驱动的云原生企业可靠性框架，通过集成机器学习模型、Kubernetes编排、可观测性管道和自动化事件响应机制，提升预测分析、智能DevOps自动化和系统韧性，实验表明可改善运营效率、减少停机时间并增强可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式基础设施日益复杂，传统的可靠性工程实践难以应对动态环境中的故障预测、异常检测和自动化运维需求，亟需将AI与DevOps方法论相结合以实现自适应和自主的基础设施管理。

Method: 该框架整合了机器学习模型、Kubernetes编排、可观测性管道以及自动化事件响应机制，涵盖预测性故障检测、异常监控、自愈基础设施和CI/CD优化等方面，形成一套完整的云原生企业可靠性解决方案。

Result: 实验评估表明该框架能够提升企业环境的运营效率，降低系统停机时间，并增强系统的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了将人工智能与DevOps方法论相结合的实际路径，为分布式基础设施实现自适应且自主的管理提供了一种可行的工程实践方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven cloud-native enterprise reliability framework designed to improve predictive analytics, intelligent DevOps automation, and system resilience in modern distributed infrastructures. The proposed framework integrates machine learning models, Kubernetes orchestration, observability pipelines, and automated incident response mechanisms to enhance reliability engineering practices. The study explores predictive failure detection, anomaly monitoring, self-healing infrastructure, and CI/CD optimization using cloud-native technologies. Experimental evaluation demonstrates improved operational efficiency, reduced downtime, and enhanced scalability for enterprise environments. The framework provides a practical approach for combining artificial intelligence with DevOps methodologies to achieve adaptive and autonomous infrastructure management.

</details>

Result: 识别出29项事件日志生成中的挑战，这些挑战可由5类基本需求来应对；现有解决方案已部分融入数据工程和领域知识工程的技术手段。

Abstract: Process mining is becoming an essential technology that not only supports the improvement of business processes but also enables the software technologies underlying the future digital economy. Among the multiple challenges discussed by the process mining community, data quality is emerging as the most pressing one. However, insufficient attention has been paid to event log generation as the very first phase of process mining and to its relationship with data engineering and requirements engineering. To address this gap, we conducted a systematic literature review of data-related challenges, requirements, and solutions in event log generation. Our results show that a wide range of 29 challenges can be addressed with 5 basic categories of requirements. Existing solutions already embed some form of data engineering and domain knowledge engineering techniques for event log generation. This review can support researchers and practitioners in understanding current trends in event log generation for process mining and in leveraging data and requirements engineering techniques to improve log generation and, ultimately, process mining outcomes.

Result: 文章为进行中研究的阶段性报告：呈现了第一场讨论会（ICSE）中识别的SA研究大问题及其讨论结果，第二场讨论会（ICSA）的结果将在后续工作中补充呈现。

Conclusion: 本文是一个社区倡议的初步成果报告，旨在为SA研究社区确立研究方向上的大问题优先级，区分真正值得研究的大问题与短暂的技术趋势。工作仍在进行中，后续将以第二场讨论会的结果进行完善。

Abstract: Software Architecture (SA) is more important than ever, from the need to master the pervasive impact of technology in society and planet earth, to balancing the role of humans versus technology, flexibility versus chaos, economic convenience versus digital autonomy. Despite SA importance, we feel that the SA research community needs to reflect on what are the big questions that deserve scientific research, and what are instead technology trends or hypes. In this paper, we report the results of a community initiative aiming to identify and discuss the big questions in SA research. Organised in two sessions hosted in 2026 at the ICSE and ICSA conferences, respectively, this paper reports on the results collected during the first session, and looks forward to complementing them with the results of the second one. To be continued ...

</details>


### [19] [Toward Model-Driven Digital Twin Configuration: Separating Structure Semantics and Runtime with SysML SAREF and Ditto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04213)
*Andrey Sadovykh,Matthew Rusakov,Kirill Korikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张将能源社区数字孪生配置任务拆分为三种互补工具：SysML（系统结构建模）、SAREF4ENER（能源语义词汇标准）和 Eclipse Ditto（基于 W3C Web-of-Things 的数字孪生运行平台），并给出从带语义标注的 SysML 模型推导 Ditto 配置的概念性规则，以提高可追溯性和配置一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前能源社区数字孪生的配置大多是手动完成，工程师手写配置存在字段名称随意、单位隐式、缺乏设备间关系的机器可读描述等问题，导致其他系统无法可靠地解释这些配置，阻碍了系统间的互操作性。

Method: 将传统的手写配置任务拆分为三个专业化工具的协作：SysML 负责系统结构建模、SAREF4ENER 提供标准能量词汇表以确保语义明确、Eclipse Ditto（经由 W3C Web-of-Things Thing Description）承担运行时数字孪生表示。作者给出了从标注了 SAREF4ENER 词汇的 SysML 模型推导出 Ditto 配置的概念性规则（尚非可执行的生成器），并在 Modelio 中对温室能源社区节点实例进行了说明。

Result: 作者认为该拆分方法相比手写配置方案，能够改善可追溯性（traceability）和配置一致性（configuration consistency），并为互操作性建立语义钩子（semantic hooks）。论文将贡献范围限定在设计时（design-time）配置，并讨论了其局限性。

Conclusion: 本文倡导一种模型驱动的、语义增强的数字孪生配置范式，通过工具职责分离使各组件发挥所长，为能源社区数字孪生的自动化配置与跨系统互操作奠定概念基础，但规则仍有待进一步实现为可执行的代码生成工具。

Abstract: Energy communities, neighbourhoods that jointly produce, store, and consume electricity, increasingly rely on \emph{digital twins}: live software copies of physical devices such as a solar panel, a battery, or a heater, kept in sync for monitoring and control. Setting up such a twin is today mostly manual: an engineer hand-writes a configuration whose field names are arbitrary, whose units are implicit, and which carries no machine-readable description of how devices relate, so other systems cannot reliably interpret it. This position paper argues that the task should be split across three complementary tools, each doing what it does best: a modelling language for the system's structure (SysML), a standard energy vocabulary for its meaning (SAREF4ENER), and a digital-twin platform for the running representation (Eclipse Ditto, via the W3C Web-of-Things Thing Description). We give conceptual rules (not yet an executable generator) for deriving a Ditto configuration from a SysML model annotated with that vocabulary, illustrate them on a greenhouse energy-community node modelled in Modelio, and argue that the split improves traceability and configuration consistency, and establishes the semantic hooks needed for interoperability, over hand-written alternatives. We scope the contribution to design-time configuration and discuss its limits.

</details>


### [20] [A Mixed-Method Empirical Study of LLM Assistance in Software Engineering Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04214)
*Pamali D. Weerasinghe,Roshan N. Rajapakse,Isuru Dharmadasa,Chamath Keppitiyagama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法实证研究两个不同年级（大一与大四）本科生的LLM辅助软件工程行为。研究分为初步调查（n=157）和任务型准实验（n=20），对比AI辅助与非AI条件下的实现、约束驱动算法选择和架构推理三类任务表现及其行为轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM在软件开发中的影响讨论往往未区分任务类型、开发者资历和验证需求，缺乏细粒度的实证理解。

Method: 采用混合方法设计：第一阶段为基础调查（n=157）刻画两组的LLM暴露度、依赖度及信任校准；第二阶段为基于任务的准实验（n=20），设置AI辅助与非AI条件，覆盖实现、约束驱动算法选择及架构推理任务，结合屏幕录制行为追踪与定性编码分析。

Result: 调查显示LLM普及率高且存在大量验证工作，两组在约束密集型场景中对LLM能力的感知存在差异。准实验显示AI辅助改变了工作流结构：参与者倾向于AI优先任务进入、复制粘贴集成及AI中介调试，而非AI工作流更依赖文档、已有模板和迭代试错。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的收益具有任务依赖性，受开发者专长和验证实践的中介作用影响，而非单纯由生成速度决定。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, yet their effects are often discussed without distinguishing between task types, developer seniority, and verification demands. This paper presents a mixed-method empirical study of LLM-assisted software engineering with first-year and fourth-year undergraduates. Phase 1 is a preliminary survey (n=157) that characterizes LLM exposure, reliance, and trust calibration among the two groups. Phase 2 is a task-based quasi-experiment with a purposive sample from both cohorts (n=20). Here, we compare AI-assisted and non-AI conditions on a structured set of software engineering tasks spanning implementation, constraint-driven algorithm selection, and architectural reasoning. We then analyze performance outcomes alongside behavioral traces captured via screen recording and a qualitative coding process. Survey results indicate widespread LLM adoption and substantial verification effort, alongside cohort differences in perceived LLM capability for constraint-heavy scenarios. The quasi-experiment further shows that AI assistance changes workflow structure. For example, participants frequently adopt AI-first task entry, copy-transfer integration, and AI-mediated debugging, whereas non-AI workflows rely more on documentation, prior templates, and iterative trial-error refinement. Overall, our findings suggest that the benefits of LLM assistance are task-dependent and mediated by expertise and verification practices, rather than by generation speed alone.

</details>


### [21] [A systematic literature review on logging smell detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04215)
*Nora Madi,Manal Binkhonain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述（SLR）对21项关于日志坏味道检测的研究进行了梳理，定义了关键术语、将坏味道类型映射到现有分类体系，并分析了检测技术、数据集和评估策略。研究发现该领域研究分散且不一致，缺乏统一基准和标准化评估方法，最后指出未来方向包括开发更好的工具、利用大语言模型以及构建标准化数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志在软件开发中至关重要，但不良的日志记录实践会引入日志坏味道（logging smells），降低日志价值甚至带来问题。然而现有研究对日志坏味道检测的关注缺乏系统梳理，作者希望通过系统文献综述来全面理解自动检测技术、数据集和评估方法的现状，为领域提供清晰的图景。

Method: 采用系统文献综述（SLR）方法，筛选并深入分析了21篇聚焦日志坏味道检测的研究。具体包括：定义关键的日志相关术语；识别不同类型的坏味道并将其映射到现有分类体系；系统审查各研究采用的检测技术、所用数据集以及评估策略。

Result: 研究发现该领域研究仍处于分散和不一致状态：缺少通用的基准测试集和标准化的评估方法，导致研究之间难以比较；同时，不同研究在针对的坏味道类型和数量上存在不一致，缺乏统一处理方式。

Conclusion: 日志坏味道的研究和检测仍有较大改进空间。作者指出了若干挑战，并建议未来研究方向包括：开发更完善的检测工具、探索大语言模型（LLMs）在该任务中的应用，以及建立更标准化的评估数据集。

Abstract: Context:Logging is an important part of software development that helps developers monitor systems, understand behavior, and fix problems. But when logging is done poorly, it can introduce logging smells, which are defects that reduce the usefulness of logs or even make them problematic. Objective:This study looks at how logging smells are currently detected. The goal is to better understand the existing research on automatic detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation methods. Method:We conducted a systematic literature review (SLR) of 21 studies focused on detecting logging smells. In this review, we define key logging-related terms, identify and map the types of smells to an existing taxonomy, and examine the detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation strategies used across the studies. Results:We found that the research is still scattered and inconsistent. For example, there is no common benchmark or standardized approach for evaluating results, making it difficult to compare studies. In addition, we observe inconsistencies in the way log smells are addressed, as studies differ in the types and number of smells they target. Conclusion:There is still room for improvement in how logging smells are studied and detected. We point out several challenges and suggest future directions, such as developing better tools, using large language models (LLMs), and building more standardized datasets for evaluation.

</details>


### [22] [Engineering as Code: Bringing Software Engineering Methodology to Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04216)
*Song Difei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"Engineering as Code (EaC)"范式，将工程设计表达为文本原生的声明式语言，通过自动化规则引擎、版本控制和包管理作为质量门，并定义ADL三子语言（PDL/PML/PLL）与ESA静态分析来替代传统基于几何的合规检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成、芯片设计等可验证领域进步显著，但在建筑、机械工程、暖通等工程设计任务上仍受限。作者认为根因并非模型能力不足，而是缺乏可计算的设计表示（"Design as Code"基础）。现有CAD/BIM系统将设计逻辑与几何耦合导致QA后移；ACC存在几何误报和命名依赖的重建开销。

Method: 提出EaC范式，核心包括：(1)信息表示假设——工程AI瓶颈源于缺乏可计算设计表示；(2)ADL（装配定义语言），以Part为原子，划分为PDL（部件定义）、PML（部件配合）、PLL（部件布局）三个正交子语言；(3)ESA（工程静态分析），将合规检查从下游审查前移到设计阶段，基于语义类别（族类型、配合关系）而非几何操作。

Result: 原型验证了ADL表达力和ESA检测能力，在三个跨领域样本（电信机架扩展、模块化数据中心、机械键盘）上200ms内检查69条规则；控制违规注入实验实现100%检出率、零假阳性。SD-HWE-Bench基准设计已完成作为实证测试平台。

Conclusion: 工程设计AI的瓶颈在于缺失可计算的机器可消费、可验证、可版本化的设计表示，而非模型能力。通过EaC范式将设计表达为文本原生声明式语言，可消除ACC的几何误报与命名依赖问题，实现设计时合规检查。

Abstract: Large language models have made significant progress in verifiable domains such as code generation and chip design, yet remain limited on engineering design tasks in fields like architecture, mechanical engineering, and HVAC. This paper argues the root cause is not model capability but the absence of a "Design as Code" computable foundation: design representations that can be consumed, verified, and versioned by machines. Existing CAD/BIM systems couple design logic with geometry, causing quality assurance to drift downstream; Automated Compliance Checking (ACC) suffers from geometric false positives and naming-dependent model reconstruction overhead. We propose the Engineering as Code (EaC) paradigm: expressing engineering design as text-native declarative language with automated rule engines, version control, and package management as quality gates. Core contributions: (1) the Information Representation Hypothesis, arguing the engineering AI bottleneck stems from missing computable design representations rather than model capability; (2) ADL (Assembly Definition Language) with Part as atom, organized into three orthogonal sub-languages -- PDL (Part Definition), PML (Part Mating), and PLL (Part Layout); (3) ESA (Engineering Static Analysis), shifting compliance checking from downstream review to design-time, operating on semantic categories (Family types, Mate relations) rather than geometry, eliminating false positives and naming dependencies. A prototype validates ADL expressiveness and ESA detection across three cross-domain samples (telecom rack expansion, modular datacenter, mechanical keyboard), checking 69 rules in under 200ms. A controlled violation injection experiment achieves 100% detection rate with zero false positives. The SD-HWE-Bench benchmark design is complete as an empirical testbed for the hypothesis.

</details>


### [23] [A Governance Methodology Layer for AI-Assisted Software Development: Defect Taxonomy, Controlled Ablation, and Process-Over-Capability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04218)
*Sungjin Kwon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自主编码智能体"语法正确但语义不正确"的问题，提出了一种基于权限与治理模块的缺陷分类体系、运行时解耦的治理门控协议、方法论即代码的形式化框架，并通过受控消融实验证明评审流程设计（而非模型能力）是决定关键缺陷覆盖率的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码智能体以高速产出能通过编译、类型检查和CI的结构化正确代码，但句法正确不等于语义正确——设计边界、安全不变量和维护性契约对自动化流水线而言仍"结构不可见"，缺乏将语义级审查纳入代码生成流程的系统性方法。

Method: 四个贡献：(1) 基于五个AI智能体权限与治理模块的缺陷类别分类体系，区分静态分析可检测与需语义审查的缺陷；(2) 运行时解耦的治理门控——基于文件系统的协议，读取生成器输出并发出结构化判定，无API耦合，跨代码生成工具可移植；(3) 方法论即代码——以版本化、可执行、跨平台物件的两层架构表达验证协议，分离可移植方法论与主机特定自动化；(4) 受控消融实验（E-ablation，N=5工件，8项独立真值），对比结构化评审与Token匹配的非结构化评审提示。

Result: 结构化条件宽松真值召回率62%对非结构化50%，严格召回率25%对0%，且严重程度分级更一致（非结构化条件夸大严重性）。两种条件均遗漏了人类QA评审员发现的文档质量缺陷，表明结构化AI评审与人工流程检查具有互补性。

Conclusion: 在样本范围内，结果支持"评审流程设计而非评审模型能力，是AI辅助软件开发中关键缺陷覆盖率的主导因素"这一论点，暗示治理与流程架构对语义质量的保障作用大于底层模型本身的能力上限。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents produce output that passes syntactic checks -- compilation, type safety, CI -- at high velocity. Yet syntactic correctness does not imply semantic correctness: design boundaries, security invariants, and maintainability contracts remain structurally invisible to automated pipelines. This paper makes four contributions toward closing this gap. First, we present a defect-class taxonomy grounded in five AI agent permission and governance modules, distinguishing defects structurally detectable by static analysis from those requiring semantic review. Second, we describe a runtime-decoupled governance gate -- a file-based protocol that reads generator output and emits a structured verdict without API coupling, making it portable across code-generation tools. Third, we formalize methodology-as-code: expressing a verification protocol as a version-controlled, executable, cross-platform artifact with a two-layer architecture separating portable methodology from host-specific automation. Fourth, we report a controlled ablation experiment (E-ablation, N=5 artifacts, 8-item independent ground truth) comparing harness-structured review against a token-matched unstructured review prompt on the same artifacts. The structured condition records 62% lenient ground-truth recall against 50% for the unstructured condition, with 25% strict recall against 0%, and applies more consistent severity grades where the unstructured condition inflates severity. Both conditions miss document-quality defects identified by a human QA reviewer, indicating a complementarity between structured AI review and human process inspection. Within the bounds of this sample, the results are consistent with the thesis that review-process design, rather than reviewer-model capability, is the dominant factor in critical-defect coverage for AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [24] [Large Language Models for Fuzz Testing in Microservices: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04219)
*Ying Song,Ke Ping,Yuqing Wang,Xiaozhou Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLM辅助的微服务模糊测试进行了系统性文献综述（SLR），分析了2024至2026年间发表的20项主要研究，提出了LLM角色与集成方式的分类体系，总结了评估实践现状，并将开放挑战映射到未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统依赖异构API，其组合性输入空间和有状态依赖使传统模糊测试面临挑战；LLM虽已被引入以增强模糊测试的语义推理能力，但相关研究缺乏系统性的梳理与综合，尚未明确LLM如何被应用、如何被评估以及存在哪些挑战。

Method: 遵循既定的SLR指南，筛选并分析了2024至2026年间发表的20项主要研究，从LLM在模糊测试中的角色定位、与现有架构的集成方式、评估指标与实践、以及遗留挑战等维度进行系统化综合。

Result: 结果显示LLM主要作为黑盒模糊测试中的语义输入生成器使用，并呈现出向基于代理（agent-based）和检索增强（RAG）架构演进的趋势，能够改善有效输入生成并在适度程度上提升覆盖率和漏洞检测能力。然而评估实践异质性明显：缺乏标准的基准测试、成本报告稀缺、实验偏向单服务而缺乏对真实多服务系统的研究。

Conclusion: 本综述提供了LLM角色与集成方式的分类学、评估实践的综合视图，以及开放挑战到研究方向的映射，为LLM驱动的微服务模糊测试的设计与部署提供支持。

Abstract: Microservice systems (MSS) increasingly rely on heterogeneous APIs whose combinatorial input space and stateful dependencies challenge traditional fuzz testing. Meanwhile, Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been introduced to enhance fuzzing with semantic reasoning over specifications, inputs, and runtime feedback. This paper presents a systematic literature review (SLR) of LLM-assisted fuzz testing for microservices to synthesise how LLMs are applied, evaluated, and what challenges remain. Following established SLR guidelines, we analyze 20 primary studies published between 2024 and 2026. Results show LLMs are mainly used as semantic input generators in black-box fuzzing, with a growing shift towards agent-based and retrieval-augmented architectures, improving valid input generation and modestly increasing coverage and vulnerability detection. However, evaluation remains heterogeneous, with limited benchmark standardization, scarce cost reporting, and a bias toward single-service experiments, highlighting a gap with real-world multi-service systems. This review provides a taxonomy of LLM roles and integrations, a consolidated view of evaluation practices, and a mapping of open challenges to research directions, supporting the design and deployment of LLM-driven fuzzing in microservices.

</details>

*Jin Wen,Yuejun Guo,Yujie Ma,Qiang Hu,Maxime Cordy*

TL;DR: 本文对七种代码大语言模型在受混淆保护代码上的翻译和补全任务进行了执行级系统研究，覆盖四种语言、五种混淆方法和三种推理协议，发现对混淆代码的直接推理结果常优于甚至等同于先反混淆再推理的结果，模型能力是首要影响因素，建议采用执行级指标评估受保护代码工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码LLM日益用于处理可能被故意混淆的软件制品（如知识产权保护、逆向工程抵抗或访问控制场景），但反混淆作为代码LLM流水线预处理步骤的有效性尚未被系统性验证。现有研究缺乏跨模型和跨保护方法的系统评估，且多依赖静态相似度而非执行级指标。

Method: 采用执行级（execution-based）评估方法，对七个代码LLM在受保护代码的翻译和补全任务上进行系统研究。实验范围覆盖C++、Go、Java、JavaScript四种源语言，五种混淆方法，以及三种推理协议（plain、obfuscated、deobfuscated）。

Result: 高能力模型（如GPT-4.1和Qwen3-Coder-30B）在混淆翻译输入上仍能保持约90%的Pass@1；直接对混淆代码推理常达到或超过对恢复后代码的推理效果。同模型反混淆虽能修复部分混淆导致的失败，但也会降低许多原本成功的案例，低能力模型净损失最大。模型能力是主导因素，源语言和混淆方法为次要但一致的效应。

Conclusion: 显式反混淆预处理在多数场景下并不必要，推荐模型感知的流水线设计；受保护代码工作流应优先使用执行级指标而非静态相似度进行评估。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used on software artifacts that may be intentionally obfuscated for intellectual-property protection, reverse-engineering resistance, or controlled access. In reverse engineering and security analysis, deobfuscation is commonly treated as the preprocessing step before downstream analysis or inference, yet its utility for code LLM pipelines has not been systematically validated across models and protection methods. We present an execution-based study of seven code LLMs on protected-code translation and completion, spanning source programs from C++, Go, Java, and JavaScript, five obfuscation methods, and three inference protocols: plain, obfuscated, and deobfuscated. Our results show that direct inference on obfuscated code often matches or exceeds inference on restored code. In this controlled benchmark, higher-capability models such as GPT-4.1 and Qwen3-Coder-30B retain about 90% Pass@1 on obfuscated translation inputs, indicating that explicit restoration is often unnecessary. Same-model restoration does recover some obfuscation-induced failures, but it also degrades many cases that already succeed, with lower-capability models showing the largest net losses. Across settings, model capability is the primary factor, while source language and obfuscation method have secondary but consistent effects. Overall, our findings support model-aware pipeline design and indicate that protected-code workflows should be evaluated primarily with execution-based metrics rather than static similarity alone.

</details>

*Konstantin Dudzik,Maximilian Kirschner,Jürgen Becker*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的自动化部署方法，通过工具链将实时应用自动适配到相位执行（phased execution）模式，以缓解多核系统中共享内存争用带来的干扰。该方法直接从生成的应用二进制中提取所需信息，对应用实现改动极小，并在RISC-V多核系统上的航空电子案例中完成了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核实时系统面临共享内存竞争导致的干扰问题。相位执行方法虽能通过互斥的内存阶段解决此问题，但管理和调度存储阶段过于复杂，阻碍了相位执行在实际实时应用中的采用。

Method: 提出一种基于模型的部署方法论，集成工具化部署流程：自动为应用扩展必要的内存阶段，并提供运行时环境在目标硬件平台上编排执行。部署工具直接从生成的应用二进制中提取所需信息，因而对应用实现的改动需求极小。

Result: 在RISC-V多核系统的航空电子案例研究中验证了该方法的可行性，展示了其关键组件与操作原理。

Conclusion: 通过自动化的模型驱动部署流程，该方法显著降低了相位执行模式在实时应用中采用的门槛，为多核实时系统共享内存干扰问题提供了实用化的解决路径。

Abstract: The increasing throughput demands in real-time systems and the consolidation of functionality on few, high-performance platforms are driving a shift towards parallel architectures. A key challenge for multicore real-time systems is the interference from contention for access to shared memory. Phased execution approaches address this challenge by restricting shared memory access to mutually exclusive memory phases, while computation is limited to memories local to each core. However, the complexity of managing and scheduling said memory phases hinders the adoption of phased execution in real-time applications. To address this challenge, we propose a model-based deployment methodology that automates the process of adapting applications to phased execution. We present an integrated tool-based deployment process that extends the application through the necessary memory phases and provides a runtime environment to orchestrate execution on the target hardware platform. Our approach requires minimal changes to the application implementation since the deployment tool directly extracts the required information from the generated application binaries. We validate our methodology using an avionics case study on a RISC-V multicore system, showcasing its key components and principles of operation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出"physics-direct"方法，将齿轮接触的闭合形式物理（接触点定位、主曲率提取、椭圆Hertz解）直接压缩为无分支的定点FPGA数据通路，替代传统的加载齿面接触分析（LTCA）LCP求解器和神经代理模型，实现实时、确定性、可嵌入的齿面接触估计。在退役XC7K480T芯片上通过开源openXC7流程验证，片上预览与LCP基准相比压力误差在-22%以内、LTE峰峰值误差在-19%以内，并且零训练即具备超越训练网络的载荷外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齿轮数字孪生、硬件在环试验台和主动振动控制需要对瞬时齿面接触状态进行闭环实时感知，而传统LTCA作为数据依赖的LCP问题每个啮合相位需fp64计算数秒，无法满足实时确定性要求；学习型代理模型虽然推理快，但每个训练样本需一次完整LTCA求解且缺乏训练覆盖范围之外的外推保证。

Method: 将接触物理的闭合形式求解过程（接触点定位、主曲率提取、椭圆Hertz解）直接压缩为分支自由的定点FPGA数据通路，包含四个系统级芯片（SoC）设计：几何、Hertz和两条融合路径。全程使用完全开源的openXC7流程实现，不依赖任何厂商工具或浮点IP，在退役的XC7K480T硅片上验证。

Result: 四个SoC通过位精确的JTAG回读与黄金模型一致。片上预览跟踪LCP体压力误差在-22%以内，加载传动误差（LTE）峰峰值误差在-19%以内，并精确保持W^{1/3}定律；零训练条件下在载荷外推上比已训练网络更准确（18.9%对比20.3%）。延迟为编译期常量（σ=0抖动），DSP受限的几何内核以约63个通道饱和FPGA结构。

Conclusion: Physics-direct为嵌入式齿面接触估计提供了一种可解释、确定性的替代方案，在保留精确物理规律的同时，克服了LTCA实时性不足和神经代理模型缺乏外推保证与可解释性的缺陷。

Abstract: Gear digital twins, hardware-in-the-loop rigs, and active vibration control close a loop around the instantaneous tooth-contact state, demanding a solver that is real-time, deterministic, and embeddable -- properties that loaded tooth contact analysis (LTCA), a data-dependent linear complementarity problem (LCP) costing seconds per mesh phase in fp64, structurally lacks; learned surrogates infer fast but spend one full LTCA solve per training sample and offer no guarantee beyond their training envelope. We instead compress the closed-form contact physics -- contact-point localization, principal-curvature extraction, and the elliptical Hertz solution -- directly into a branch-free fixed-point FPGA datapath, termed physics-direct, realized through the fully open-source openXC7 flow (no vendor tools, no floating-point IP) and validated on retired XC7K480T silicon. Four SoCs -- geometry, Hertz, and two fused paths -- pass bit-exact JTAG readback against a golden model. The on-chip preview tracks LCP body pressure to within -22% and loaded transmission error (LTE) peak-to-peak to -19%, preserving the exact $W^{1/3}$ law; with zero training it extrapolates in load more accurately than a trained network (18.9% versus 20.3%). Latency is a compile-time constant ($σ= 0$ jitter), and the DSP-bound geometry kernel saturates the fabric near 63 lanes. Physics-direct is an interpretable, deterministic alternative to neural surrogacy for embedded tooth-contact estimation.

</details>


### [28] [Reviewer Capability Governs Rejection Targeting, Not Repair Skill: Evidence from LLM Execute-Review-Revise Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04270)
*Faizan Tanveer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过固定100道奥林匹克数学题，将评审者从同族旗舰模型替换为跨家族/跨能力的多种模型，系统性地改变评审者的能力层级。研究发现：跨家族中端评审者比自评（同模型）效果更好（准确率提升12个百分点，0损坏答案），而自评虽检出率最高但误拒率高（35%误拒自己的正确答案）、修复率仅15%，且低损坏率实为"执行者不服从"的惰性假象，而非评审质量高。能力低于某个下限的评审者完全失效（0修改、双倍token成本）。作者强调这是单配置、100题的受控试验，结论不宜泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线将不同能力档次的模型分配执行和验证角色，因为全流程使用旗舰模型成本过高。已有文献表明验证阶段并非总是有益，但通常将评审者能力视为相对于执行者固定或接近。作者希望系统地改变评审者能力层级，探究评审者能力变化如何影响验证阶段的收益与损害。

Method: 在固定100道奥林匹克数学题上，保持执行者不变，将评审者替换为覆盖不同能力区间的多种模型，包括跨家族中端模型、同族同模型（自评），以及能力极低的模型（甚至完全无法解题）。逐一测量每次拒绝的最终结果，统计分析不同评审条件下的准确率变化、错误检出率、错误拒绝率、修复率等指标。

Result: （1）跨家族中端评审者使最终准确率从52%提升至64%（+12个百分点，p=0.0005），且零损坏答案；（2）同模型自评的错误检出率最高（0.85 recall），但准确率无显著提升：拒绝频率是对照组的2.1倍，修复率仅为15%（vs跨家族43%，p=0.0074），且误拒自己35%的正确回答（跨家族仅2%，p=0.000015）；（3）自评的低损害率是"修订惰性"而非评审质量：18个被误拒的正确回答中，执行者仅服从3个（全部变错），忽略其余15个才得以存活；（4）能力低于下限的评审者完全惰化，100个最终答案零改动但token成本翻倍。

Conclusion: 评审者的能力配置对验证阶段收益有决定性影响。跨家族异构中端评审者可能优于同模型自评——后者的问题不在于检不出错误，而在于过度拒绝、修复率低且容易误伤正确解答；自评表面上的低损害率实质上源于执行者的不服从（修订惰性）这一虚假安全。同时存在评审能力下限，低于此限的评审角色完全失效。但本研究属于单一配置、100道题的受控试点研究，不应作为关于验证阶段的普遍结论来解读。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines increasingly assign roles, including execution and verification, to models of different capability tiers. This is done because running a flagship model at every stage is expensive. Previous literature has established that verification stages are not always beneficial, but holds reviewer capability roughly fixed relative to the executor. We vary it. We replace the reviewer with models spanning a capability range down to one that cannot solve the problems at all, and measure the outcome of every individual rejection. This is done across a constant set of 100 olympiad mathematics problems.
  A cross-family mid-tier reviewer improves final accuracy by 12 percentage points, from 52 to 64 percent (p = 0.0005), with zero damaged answers. Same-model self-review attains the highest error-detection rate of any condition (0.85 recall) yet yields no significant gain: it rejects 2.1 times as often for a third the repair rate (15 against 43 percent, p = 0.0074) and falsely rejects 35 percent of its own correct answers against 2 percent for the cross-family reviewer (paired p = 0.000015). The low damage rate of self-review proves to be an artifact of revision inertia rather than reviewer quality: of 18 falsely rejected correct answers, the three where the executor complied all became wrong, while the fifteen it ignored survived unchanged. Below a capability floor the role becomes inert: our weakest reviewer changed zero of 100 final answers while doubling token cost. These findings describe a single executor-reviewer configuration on 100 problems and should be read as a controlled pilot rather than a general claim about verification stages.

</details>


### [29] [Integrating Crash Report Mining and LLMs for Bug Localization and Repair: An Industrial Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04483)
*Marcos Medeiros,Uirá Kulesza,Christoph Treude,Daniel Lucena,Rafael Gomes,Roberta Coelho,Eiji Adachi,Rodrigo Bonifacio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文调查了将崩溃报告挖掘技术（堆栈跟踪聚类、可疑文件和方法的排序）与大语言模型（LLMs）结合，以支持工业环境中崩溃定位和修复的有效性。在两个大型Java企业系统的38个崩溃bug上进行了回顾性评估，最佳配置能定位71%的崩溃bug并正确修复52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业软件系统中崩溃报告bug的分析需要大量维护工作，尤其是在生产环境中处理大量崩溃报告和源代码工件的场景。虽然已有研究表明LLM可以协助维护任务，但对其在工业环境中支持开发者分析崩溃报告bug和修复与崩溃报告组相关的bug的有效性知之甚少。

Method: 将崩溃报告挖掘技术（堆栈跟踪聚类、可疑文件和方法的排名）与LLM集成，以支持崩溃定位和修复。在四种提示配置下对五个LLM进行回顾性评估，选出最佳模型后在从两个大型Java企业系统收集的38个崩溃bug上进行测试，并通过人工验证评估LLM生成响应的结构特征、解释模式以及定位和修复效果。

Result: 最佳配置在最全数据集上能定位高达71%的崩溃bug并正确修复52%的崩溃bug。

Conclusion: 将崩溃报告挖掘与基于LLM的修复相结合，可以有效地支持工业维护工作流中的调试活动，为这一研究方向提供了实证证据。

Abstract: Analyzing crash-report bugs in large-scale industrial software systems requires substantial maintenance effort, particularly in production environments where developers must handle large volumes of crash reports and source code artifacts to localize and fix their root causes. While recent studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) can assist with maintenance tasks, little is known about their effectiveness in supporting developers in analyzing crash-report bugs and repairing bugs associated with groups of crash reports in industrial settings. To address this gap, we investigate whether integrating crash report mining techniques---specifically stack trace clustering and suspicious file and method ranking---with LLMs can support crash localization and repair in production environments. We conduct a retrospective evaluation of five LLMs under four prompt configurations. After that, we chose the best model to run on 38 crash bugs collected from two large Java enterprise systems. We further analyze the structural characteristics and explanatory patterns of LLM-generated responses and assess localization and repair effectiveness through manual validation. Our results show that the best-performing configuration localizes up to 71% and correctly repairs 52% of crash bugs on the full dataset. These findings provide empirical evidence that combining crash report mining with LLM-based repair can effectively support debugging activities in industrial maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [30] [An Empirical Analysis of CodeQL False Positives and Query Refinements for Java Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04535)
*Amirali Sajadi,Saikat Dutta,Preetha Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析CodeQL在Java安全分析中的167个CVE实例和500个误报样本，构建了包含5类误报模式的源码级分类体系，并基于此实现CodeQL查询级精化，可移除81.8%的已审查误报。同时探索了agentic编码工具自动适配精化模式到新项目的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具（如CodeQL）能帮助开发者在部署前发现漏洞，但大量误报导致显著的分类/分流工作负担。研究旨在系统地理解CodeQL Java安全分析中误报是否形成可重复、可解释的模式，以及能否通过精化分析来减少误报。

Method: 在110个项目的167个CVE实例上运行CodeQL的Java安全查询套件，聚焦误报率最高的十个查询。人工审查500个采样误报路径和位置，构建源码级误报分类体系（五类：缺失路径约束或净化36.6%、良性执行上下文29.4%、缺失信任边界建模27.6%、不精确并发建模5%、不精确sink建模1.4%）。基于分类实现CodeQL查询级精化，并评估agentic编码工具是否能在模板引导下将精化模式适配到新项目。

Result: 精化移除了81.8%的已审查误报；在完整选定查询数据集上移除15.8%的报道路径和位置，同时保留7/8的真实阳性。将精化模式作为模板提供给agentic编码工具时，两个工具分别有56%和62%的任务成功率，查询编译通过率超过90%；无模板引导时两工具成功率仅为28%，编译率降至30-36%。

Conclusion: 研究支持一种以精化为导向的SAST工作流：将重复出现的误报建模在CodeQL查询中，并自动适配到不同项目上下文，从而减少重复性的人工分流工作。同时指出固定精化模式通常依赖项目特定上下文，存在泛化差距，agentic工具可在模板引导下部分弥合这一差距。

Abstract: Static application security testing (SAST) tools help developers find vulnerabilities before deployment, but false positives create substantial triage effort. We study whether CodeQL false positives in Java security analysis form recurring, explainable patterns that can be reduced by refining the analysis. We run CodeQL's Java security query suite on 167 CVE instances from 110 projects, focusing on the ten queries with the highest false positive rates. We manually review 500 sampled false positive paths and locations and construct a source-level taxonomy. The five categories are Missed Path Constraint or Sanitization (36.6%), Benign Execution Context (29.4%), Missing Trust Boundary Modeling (27.6%), Imprecise Concurrency Modeling (5%), and Imprecise Sink Modeling (1.4%).
  Guided by these findings, we implement CodeQL refinements that detect and filter recurring false positive patterns at the query level. The refinements remove 81.8% of reviewed false positives. Across the full selected-query dataset, they remove 15.8% of reported paths and locations while retaining 7 of 8 true positives. This shows that many false positives can be reduced in the analysis, although fixed refinements often depend on project-specific context. To address this generalization gap, we evaluate whether agentic coding tools can adapt refinement patterns to new projects. Given our patterns as templates, the two tools succeed on 56% and 62% of tasks, with query compile-pass rates above 90%. Without this guidance, both succeed on only 28%, while compile rates fall to 30-36%. These results support a refinement-oriented SAST workflow in which recurring false positives are modeled in CodeQL queries and automatically adapted to different project contexts, reducing repeated triage.

</details>

*Gnaneswar Villuri,Hashmath Shaik,Alex Doboli*

TL;DR: 本文提出动态上下文适应（dynamic context adaptation），一种面向"静态绑定"问题的样本高效的验证-生成循环方法。该问题指代码生成正确性依赖于组件间运行时耦合（一个例程的含义由另一例程的运行时行为定义），无法仅从文本描述解析。方法通过验证代理从执行轨迹提取结构化诊断信息作为类梯度引导，结合问题描述的知识图谱提供语义约束，并用模拟退火选择候选避免贪心坍塌。在八个问题中的七个上，在300次和600次评估时显著优于零样本、Reflexion和OpenEvolve（p<0.01）；在主激励问题（跨耦合优化）上在1000次评估时也取得最佳分数。消融实验证实结构化执行反馈是主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成方法在处理依赖执行时耦合的代码生成任务时存在根本局限，这种"静态绑定"问题无法仅凭文本描述解决，且在打包、路由、符号搜索等广泛场景中不同程度存在。作者希望填补这一方法空白。

Method: 提出动态上下文适应框架：(1) 验证代理从执行轨迹中提取结构化诊断信息，充当类似梯度的引导信号；(2) 生成代理在每次迭代中基于验证反馈和来自问题描述知识图谱的语义约束提出多个候选；(3) 使用模拟退火在候选中进行选择，以避免贪心坍塌导致的局部最优。

Result: 在八个问题中的七个上，该方法在300次和600次评估预算下显著优于零样本、Reflexion和OpenEvolve（p<0.01），此预算范围内基于种群搜索的方法尚无法积累足够多样性与之竞争。在主激励的跨耦合优化问题上，方法在1000次评估时也获得最佳分数。消融实验确认结构化执行反馈是性能提升的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 结构化执行反馈对依赖运行时耦合的正确性判断最为有效，支持了核心假设；该方法在样本有限（评估预算小）的高联耦代码生成场景中表现优异，为该类问题提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation fails when correctness depends on execution-dependent coupling: the meaning of one routine is defined by the runtime behavior of another, a relationship that cannot be resolved from textual descriptions alone. This limitation, which we call static binding, is not confined to explicitly coupled problems; it appears to varying degrees whenever correctness depends on joint execution behavior across components, from explicit cross-coupled optimizers to subtler joint constraints in packing, routing, and symbolic search. This paper proposes dynamic context adaptation, a sample-efficient validation-generation loop designed for this setting. A validation agent extracts structured diagnostic information from execution traces, providing gradient-like guidance to a generation agent that proposes multiple candidates per iteration. A knowledge graph derived from the problem description supplies semantic constraints to the generation agent. Simulated annealing selects among candidates to avoid greedy collapse. Our method outperforms zero-shot, Reflexion, and OpenEvolve on seven of eight problems at both 300 and 600 evaluations (p < 0.01), a regime where population-based search has not yet accumulated sufficient diversity to compete. Notably, on the primary motivating problem (cross-coupled optimization), our method also achieves the best score at 1000 evaluations, consistent with the hypothesis that structured execution feedback is most beneficial when correctness depends on runtime coupling. Ablation results confirm that structured execution feedback is the primary driver.

</details>

*Zhongjie Wang,Mingyi Liu*
