TL;DR: 本文提出了一个测试性框架，用于治理由软件代理（agent）扩展的数字执行。框架包含两个核心构念（可信变更TC和责任制拓扑）及一个执行抽象（人-代理单元HAC），旨在回答当执行可被规模化复制和并行化时，组织应如何治理这些变化，使其可接受且可持续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件代理使数字执行具有弹性——代码生成、测试、迁移等可在不按比例增加人力的情况下复制和并行化；但问题框定、语义承诺、验证、集成、注意力与残余风险接受仍受限于人类认知、组织权威和经济能力。因此亟需一个治理框架来回答：组织应如何治理可扩展执行，以便接受并维持其带来的变化。

Method: 提出一个由两个构念和一个执行抽象组成的可测试框架：（1）可信变更（Trustworthy Change, TC）作为从意图经由委托执行、验证、集成、接受直至运营的工程对象；（2）责任制拓扑（Responsibility Topology）根据独立残余风险接受权威的分布对组织分类——单中心拓扑只有一个最终基线责任锚点，多锚点拓扑要求跨独立治理域的联合接受；（3）人-代理单元（Human-Agent Cell, HAC）负责产出候选方案、提议和证据，但执行不授予接受权威。作者将权威作为显式分类轴，推导其对变更状态、共享工程事实、验证和流控制的后果。

Result: 该框架揭示：分布式HAC在扩展时会产生情境一致性和失效（invalidation）压力，而多锚点治理则增加联合接受与显式责任闭合需求。文章贡献在于理论构建和操作化，但明确指出概念（如渐进规范化和有界容量分析）尚属待验证的假设，经验有效性需通过受控、纵向和现场研究进一步确认。

Conclusion: 责任、问责、变更管理、规范、验证与人类监督并非本文的原创贡献；其核心主张是——代理规模化执行改变了这些要素相互衔接的方式。本文的创新在于将责任权威提升为明确的分类轴，并推导其治理后果。理论有效性与实践适用性仍待实证检验。

Abstract: Software agents make digital execution elastic: repository analysis, code generation, testing, migration, tool use, and operations can be replicated and parallelized without proportional human headcount. Problem framing, semantic commitment, verification, integration, attention, and residual-risk acceptance remain bounded by human cognition, organizational authority, and economic capacity. How should scalable execution be governed so organizations can accept and sustain its changes?
  Our testable framework has two constructs and one execution abstraction. Trustworthy Change (TC) is the engineering object moving from intent through delegated execution, verification, integration, acceptance, and operation. Responsibility Topology classifies organizations by the distribution of independent residual-risk acceptance authority. A single-center topology has one final baseline responsibility anchor; a multi-anchor topology requires joint acceptance across independently governed domains. The Human-Agent Cell (HAC) produces candidates, proposals, and evidence; execution grants no acceptance authority. As execution and authority scale differently, distributed HACs create context-coherence and invalidation pressures, while multi-anchor governance adds joint acceptance and explicit responsibility closure.
  Responsibility, accountability, change management, specification, verification, and human oversight predate this work; our claim is only that agent-scaled execution changes how they fit together. We make that authority an explicit classification axis and derive consequences for change state, shared engineering facts, verification, and flow control. Progressive Specification and bounded-capacity analysis remain hypotheses to test, not laws. We contribute theory construction and operationalization; empirical validity remains open to controlled, longitudinal, and field studies.

</details>


### [33] [How Developers Discuss Generative AI: A Longitudinal Study of the Visual Studio Code Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04680)
*Panida Rumriankit,Akito Monden,Hiroki Inayoshi,Pattara Leelaprute,Bundit Manaskasemsak,Kundjanasith Thonglek,Arnon Rungsawang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对VS Code GitHub仓库中2021年1月至2026年6月间的43,806个候选issue进行纵向分析，经关键词检索与语义相关性过滤后获得25,227个AI相关issue。通过BERTopic主题建模与Mann-Kendall趋势检验发现：开发者讨论主要集中于AI辅助开发环境的操作性问题（代理管理、配置、可靠性、认证、计费），而调查研究中常强调的风险（如幻觉、许可问题）在issue tracker中很少出现；话题从AI辅助代码补全向对话式和代理式开发演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具已成为日常软件开发的一部分，但主流开源社区在实践中如何讨论这些工具仍缺乏系统研究。现有知识多来自调查问卷，缺少对真实开发者工作场景中讨论内容的实证分析。

Method: 从VS Code GitHub仓库收集43,806个候选issue，结合关键词检索与语义相关性过滤得到25,227个AI相关issue作为语料库；采用BERTopic进行主题识别，使用过滤后的语料进行主题验证和鲁棒性再聚类；通过月度流行率分析和Mann-Kendall趋势检验研究主题随时间演变。

Result: 开发者讨论以AI辅助开发环境的实际操作问题为主导，包括代理管理、配置、可靠性、认证和计费；调查研究中常被强调的风险（如幻觉、许可）在此场景中很少被提及；讨论内容从AI辅助代码补全向对话式和代理式开发模式演变，反映了生成式AI在开发工作流中集成程度的加深。

Conclusion: GitHub Issues提供了以实际工作流为导向的生成式AI视角，与基于调查问卷的开发者感知研究形成互补。开源社区的AI讨论主要聚焦于AI辅助软件开发的运营层面而非风险层面。

Abstract: Generative AI tools such as GitHub Copilot, ChatGPT, and coding agents have rapidly become part of everyday software development, yet little is known about how mainstream open source communities discuss them in practice. This paper presents a longitudinal analysis of generative-AI-related discussions in the Visual Studio Code (VS Code) GitHub repository, using 43,806 candidate issues created between January 2021 and June 2026. To improve corpus quality, we combined keyword retrieval with semantic relevance filtering, yielding a filtered corpus of 25,227 AI-related issues. We applied BERTopic to the retrieved corpus to identify discussion topics, using the filtered corpus for theme validation and a robustness re-clustering, and analyzed their evolution over time using monthly prevalence and Mann-Kendall trend tests. The results show that developer discussions are dominated by practical concerns regarding the operation of AI-assisted development environments, including agent management, configuration, reliability, authentication, and billing, whereas risks frequently emphasized in survey-based studies, such as hallucination and licensing, rarely surface in this venue. This suggests that discussions of generative AI in the VS Code issue tracker primarily focus on operational aspects of AI-assisted software development. Furthermore, discussions evolved from AI-assisted code completion toward conversational and agent-based development, reflecting the increasing integration of generative AI into software development workflows. These findings suggest that GitHub Issues provide a practical, workflow-oriented perspective on generative AI that complements survey-based studies of developer perceptions.

</details>


### [34] [Beyond Code Generation: Reliability, Verification, and Cost Economics in the Agentic Software Development Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04681)
*Happy Bhati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了2024至2026年间AI编码系统从自动补全/聊天演变为自主智能体（可检视仓库、编辑多文件、运行工具、编写测试、开PR并长时间低监督工作）后，软件开发流程瓶颈从"写代码"转向"审查、集成、测试、安全、部署与生产运维"的问题。论文不提出新模型实验，而是综合同行评审的软件工程研究、大学研究、基准审计、大型科技公司生产报告、开发者遥测及成本管理证据，提出四个核心工程概念：Agentic SDLC吞吐悖论、生产合格变更（PQC）、验证税、以及Agentic SDLC控制平面，并将研究问题从"智能体能生成多少代码"转向"工程系统每单位成本、审阅小时和运营风险能交付多少生产合格价值"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现场研究显示AI编码智能体在编码活动层面带来显著收益，但这些收益在从编写代码到交付可靠软件之间急剧衰减。审查、集成、测试、安全、部署和生产运维仍是约束阶段，且经济模式正从可预测的人均许可费转向可变的token、工具、沙箱、CI和返工成本。这促使作者重新审视AI时代的软件交付瓶颈与成本结构。

Method: 本文为证据综合研究（synthesis study），不涉及新模型实验。作者系统性地整合了同行评审软件工程研究、大学实验、基准审计、科技巨头生产报告、开发者遥测和成本管理证据（主要为2024至2026年9月期间），基于这些证据提出四个原创工程概念框架：1）Agentic SDLC吞吐悖论；2）生产合格变更（PQC）指标；3）验证税概念；4）Agentic SDLC控制平面架构——用于在成本、可靠性和人工注意力预算之间分配智能体自主权。

Result: 研究结论指出：AI智能体在生成代码数量上的能力已非瓶颈，真正的瓶颈是生产合格交付能力。核心发现包括编码收益在软件交付下游环节大幅衰减、成本结构从固定许可转向变量化运营成本等。作者提出了从当前受监督智能体演进到未来受策略约束的软件工厂的证据驱动路线图，并将核心研究问题重新定义为实现单位成本、审阅人时和运营风险下的最大化生产合格价值。

Conclusion: AI编码系统的发展重心应从"代码生成能力"转向"整个软件开发生命周期的生产合格交付能力"。需要建立Agentic SDLC控制平面来系统地管理和分配智能体的自主权，并引入PQC和验证税等度量概念来量化真实工程产出与验证开销。未来的研究方向是从受监督智能体转向受策略约束的软件工厂，在成本、可靠性与人力注意力等多重约束下优化软件交付。

Abstract: AI coding systems are moving from autocomplete and chat toward agents that can inspect repositories, edit multiple files, run tools, write tests, open pull requests, and work for long periods with limited supervision. This capability changes the bottleneck in software delivery. Recent field studies show meaningful gains in coding activity, but newer evidence also shows that those gains attenuate sharply between writing code and shipping reliable software. Review, integration, testing, security, deployment, and production operations remain constraining stages, while the economics are shifting from predictable per-seat licensing toward variable token, tool, sandbox, CI, and rework costs.
  This paper synthesizes peer-reviewed software-engineering research, university studies, benchmark audits, production reports from major technology companies, developer telemetry, and cost-management evidence released primarily from 2024 through September 2026. No new model experiment is claimed; numerical findings remain attributed to their original studies. The synthesis proposes four engineering concepts: the Agentic SDLC Throughput Paradox, Production-Qualified Change (PQC), the Verification Tax, and an Agentic SDLC Control Plane that allocates autonomy subject to cost, reliability, and human-attention budgets. An evidence-based horizon then maps today's supervised agents to future policy-bounded software factories. The central research question shifts from how much code an agent can generate to how much production-qualified value an engineering system can deliver per dollar, per reviewer-hour, and per unit of operational risk.

</details>


### [35] [Building a research-software catalog with a coding agent: from hackathon prototype to public deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04711)
*Kazuyoshi Yoshimi,Satoshi Terasaki,Gotai Yamada*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在生成式AI与编码代理加速研究软件开发背景下，构建可靠AI辅助软件发现门户所需的大量工程工作。通过三天黑客松开发的软件仓库目录原型及其公开部署所需的工程探索，以及将经验迁移到MateriApps检索代理的经验，发现实现可靠运行需要大量额外工程，最严重的问题不是崩溃而是产生看似合理却错误输出的静默失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和编码代理虽能加速研究软件开发，但也增加了对高效软件发现与维护的需求。现有研究软件门户缺乏AI辅助检索能力，且AI辅助系统的可靠性问题未被充分认识。

Method: 在三天的黑客松中开发仓库目录原型，并通过对抗性评审、数据质量检查、浏览器级验证和发布保障等手段评估其公开部署所需的工程改造。随后探索将经验迁移到更大规模的人工策展门户——为MateriApps开发结合策展元数据、外部文档、向量搜索与本地语言模型生成的检索代理。

Result: 编码代理实现速度快，但实现可靠运行需要大量额外工程。最严重的问题不是系统崩溃，而是静默失败——产生看似合理但内容不完整或错误的结果，其来源包括数据采集不完整、误导性评估以及检索或预处理故障。

Conclusion: AI辅助软件门户需要显式验证、监控和反复评审，策展元数据和持续维护的文档仍然不可或缺。MateriApps工作处于早期探索阶段，观察结果属初步性质；但策展元数据、自动收集文档与基于检索的辅助相结合的方式，可能对扩展其他研究软件门户具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: Generative AI and coding agents can accelerate research software development, but they also increase the need for efficient software discovery and maintenance. We developed a repository catalog during a three-day hackathon and subsequently examined the engineering required to make it suitable for public deployment, including adversarial review, data-quality checks, browser-level validation, and publication safeguards. We then explored whether the lessons learned from this prototype could be transferred to a much larger, human-curated portal, through a retrieval agent under development for MateriApps that combines curated portal metadata, external documentation, vector search, and local language-model generation. Implementation with coding agents was rapid, but achieving reliable operation required substantial additional engineering: the most consequential problems were not crashes but silent failures that produced plausible yet incomplete or incorrect outputs, arising from incomplete data acquisition, misleading assessments, and retrieval or preprocessing failures. These observations suggest that AI-assisted software portals require explicit validation, monitoring, and repeated review, and that curated metadata and maintained documentation remain essential. The MateriApps work is exploratory and remains under active development, so the observations reported for it are preliminary; a comparable combination of curated metadata, automatically collected documentation, and retrieval-based assistance may nevertheless be useful for extending other research-software portals.

</details>


### [36] [Same Request, Different Answer: Quantization Amplifies Cache-Induced Divergence in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04748)
*Aditi Patodiya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现默认启用的前缀缓存（prefix caching）会严重损害工作负载的可复现性，且该问题在低比特量化下急剧放大（16-bit下36.2%的回合轨迹改变，4-bit下达75.0%）。作者通过对照实验和消融研究，将不可复现性归因于缓存状态缺失于请求本身且默认从不重置，而非引擎的其他非确定性因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存在主流开源推理栈中默认启用并被视为透明的优化，但其对可复现性的实际代价从未被系统量化过。作者希望回答：缓存开/关是否会改变模型的输出轨迹？这种变化在多大程度上影响最终任务结果？

Method: 在固定模型、解码参数、种子和请求顺序的前提下，以batch size为1串行执行一个80回合的多轮agentic工具调用工作负载，跨2个引擎和4种权重格式，在缓存启用与禁用两种状态下运行；随后用受控缓存配置、执行顺序操作和缓存状态恢复进行三项归因实验，并增加单轮桥接实验检验对任务结果的影响。

Result: 启用缓存改变了36.2%（16-bit）和75.0%（4-bit）回合的agent轨迹；禁用缓存后800局全部bit级一致（0/800），将其他非确定性源上界定为0.5%；三项实验定位出单一server级prompt-cache设置使局间分歧移动37.5个百分点，恢复缓存状态后缓存路径与重算路径各自在40/40项上可复现却仍有14项不同。

Conclusion: 缓存服务在给定缓存状态下是确定性的，但在实践中不可复现，因为缓存状态不在请求中携带且默认从不重置。缓存对单轮任务输出有实质影响但不会改变聚合准确率；作者发布了完整harness、日志和分析管线以供社区复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: Prefix caching, in which a serving engine reuses the key and value tensors of a shared prompt prefix across requests, is enabled by default in the major open-source stacks and treated as a transparent optimization. We measure what it costs in reproducibility, and find that the cost rises sharply with weight quantization. Holding the model, decoding parameters, seed, and request order fixed, and issuing every request serially at batch size one, we ran an eighty-episode multi-turn agentic tool-use workload with caching enabled and disabled across two engines and four weight formats. Enabling the cache changed the agent's trajectory on 36.2 percent of episodes at 16-bit precision and on 75.0 percent at four-bit, a gradient that survives re-measurement under a controlled cache configuration. With caching disabled, repeated execution was bit-identical in every configuration, 0 of 800 episodes, which bounds other sources of nondeterminism at 0.5 percent. Repeated cache-enabled runs did diverge, and three experiments locate the cause: a single server-level prompt-cache setting moves run-to-run divergence by 37.5 percentage points, execution order acts only while that setting is active, and restoring cache state makes the cached and recompute paths each reproduce on 40 of 40 items while still differing from each other on 14. Cached serving is deterministic given cache state, and irreproducible in practice because that state is absent from the request and never reset by default. A single-turn bridge shows the divergence reaching task outcomes without shifting aggregate accuracy. We release the harness, logs, and analysis pipeline.

</details>

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLM在自动程序修复（APR）过程中的幻觉现象进行了多层次分析，涵盖最终补丁和中间产物（触发测试用例识别、行覆盖率预测、附加测试用例生成），在832个Defects4J bug上评估了三个代表性LLM。结果显示修复幻觉和理解幻觉普遍存在，仅21.0%-55.9%的补丁能通过开发者编写的测试套件，人工分析812个样本发现72.7%的修复存在幻觉。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 修复幻觉与理解幻觉均普遍存在：仅21.0%-55.9%的补丁通过开发者测试套件；更准确的中间产物通常与成功修复相关，但该关系不总是成立；人工分析812个样本发现72.7%的案例存在修复幻觉，其中错误因果定位占45.9%、错误修复策略占18.5%；模型频繁误识别触发测试用例、对含分支控制流的行覆盖率预测不准、生成的附加测试用例缺少bug触发条件或期望行为不正确。

Conclusion: LLM在APR过程中存在系统性的幻觉问题，且幻觉不仅限于最终补丁，还广泛发生于引导修复的中间产物中。中间产物准确度与修复成功率之间并非严格正相关，提示需要更深入地理解幻觉产生的机制，以增强LLM在程序修复中的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly advanced automated program repair (APR), yet existing evaluations remain largely result-centric and provide limited insight into hallucination during repair. In APR, hallucination may arise not only in final patches but also in the intermediate artifacts that guide patch generation. To address this gap, we perform a multi-layered analysis of hallucination throughout the APR process. Specifically, we characterize hallucination as the production of patches or intermediate artifacts that are not faithfully grounded in the available repair evidence. We examine repair hallucination in final patches and understanding hallucination in intermediate artifacts through three tasks, namely triggering testcase identification, line coverage prediction, and additional testcase generation.We then evaluate three representative LLMs on 832 Defects4J bugs through automatic evaluation and manual analysis. Our results show that both repair and understanding hallucinations remain prevalent. Across models and settings, only 21.0%-55.9% of generated patches pass the developer-written test suite. Moreover, although more accurate intermediate artifacts are generally associated with successful repairs, this relationship does not always hold. Manual analysis of 812 sampled repairs identifies repair hallucinations in 72.7% of cases, including patches that pass all available tests; incorrect causal localization and incorrect repair strategies account for 45.9% and 18.5% of these hallucinations, respectively. Meanwhile, models frequently misidentify triggering testcases, mispredict line coverage involving branching control flow, and generate additional testcases with missing bug-triggering conditions or incorrect expected behavior.

</details>


### [38] [ARIA - An Agentic Framework for Autonomous Testing of Infotainment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04913)
*António Azevedo,Bruno Lima,João Pascoal Faria*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIA（Autonomous Real-time Infotainment Assessment），一个面向Android车载信息娱乐系统的多智能体LLM框架，通过四个专职智能体构成的闭环流水线，实现基于视觉交互的端到端自动化测试。在制造商物理车载系统上30个场景的评估中，ARIA完成了28个（93.3%），其中20个（71.4%）与真实结果匹配，捕获了全部5个已知缺陷且无误报通过，验证了该框架在工业场景下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载信息娱乐系统的验证仍依赖手动测试，速度慢、成本高，且无法适应敏捷发布和OTA更新需求。现有的脚本化自动化测试将测试逻辑与实现耦合，导致测试套件脆弱且维护成本高。已有的LLM驱动测试框架主要面向Web/移动应用，且多采用单或双智能体配置，使一两个模型同时承担感知、规划、动作选择和验证任务，面对信息娱乐系统的复杂性容易出现幻觉和无意义的探索循环。

Method: ARIA采用多智能体架构，将测试流程分解为一个包含四个专职智能体的闭环流水线，每个步骤由四个智能体协作完成，外加一个报告阶段。系统从单句场景描述（路径、动作、预期结果）出发，通过视觉交互在Android信息娱乐系统上自主执行测试，并生成报告、可复现脚本和每步视觉证据。研究者还设计了单智能体基线用于对比验证多智能体设计的优越性，并区分首次通过和模型复查两个阶段分别报告结果。

Result: 在制造商物理Android信息娱乐系统上30个场景的评估中，ARIA完成28个场景（93.3%），其中2个报错；20个（71.4%）与真实标准结果匹配；捕获了全部5个已知缺陷，没有故障被误判为正常。8个误报源于导航/图像限制和不支持的手势。单智能体基线在首次通过时误报率远高（72.0% vs. 52.6%），易将导航困难与系统故障混淆；多智能体设计在更强模型复查后优势缩小。研究还报告了每场景的token数、调用次数和成本，并通过重复运行证明稳定性与复杂度相关，缺陷检测完全一致，指向CI集成视觉测试的可行性。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM框架可以工业级地运行信息娱乐系统测试，证明了基于LLM的多智能体架构在车载系统验证中的实用价值。ARIA以93.3%的完成率和100%的缺陷捕获率展示了良好的可靠性，但8个误报揭示了低误报容忍度带来的成本代价。结果同时表明该框架的稳定性与场景复杂度相关，缺陷检测的一致性使其有望集成到CI流水线中，实现视觉测试的自动化持续集成。

Abstract: Automotive infotainment validation still relies on manual testing, slow, costly, and incompatible with agile releases and OTA updates. Scripted automation only partly helps: it couples test logic to implementation, yielding brittle, high-maintenance suites. Existing LLM-driven frameworks mostly target web/mobile apps, using single- or dual-agent setups that overload one or two models with perception, planning, action selection, and validation at once, prone to hallucinations and unproductive exploration loops given infotainment complexity. We present ARIA (Autonomous Real-time Infotainment Assessment), a multi-agent LLM framework that autonomously runs end-to-end tests on Android infotainment systems via visual interaction, using a closed-loop pipeline of four specialized agents per step plus a report stage. From single-sentence scenarios (path, action, expected outcome), ARIA runs the interactions and produces reports, reproducible scripts, and visual evidence per step. Evaluated on a manufacturer's physical Android infotainment system across 30 scenarios, ARIA completed 28 (93.3%) with a verdict (2 errored), 20 of which (71.4%) matched ground truth. It caught all 5 known defects, no fault passed as working; its 8 false positives stem from navigation/image limits and unsupported gestures, showing multi-agent LLMs can run infotainment tests industrially while exposing the cost of a low false-positive tolerance. A single-agent baseline confirms the multi-agent design's value: on the first pass, before stronger-model revisitation narrows the gap, it shows a far higher false-positive rate (72.0% vs. 52.6%), conflating navigational difficulty with system failure. We report first-pass/post-revisitation results, token/call/cost per scenario, and show via repeated runs that stability tracks complexity, with fault detection perfectly consistent, pointing to CI integration of visual testing.

</details>

*Manuela Petrescu,Paul Razvan Petrescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段定性访谈研究，探讨了Scrum Master（SM）在多元文化软件公司中的学习路径与职业发展，发现SM需求的团队规模上限为六人，且行业内缺乏统一的软技能学习流程和衡量标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷开发已被业界广泛采用，对敏捷相关岗位经验丰富人才的需求持续旺盛，但针对Scrum Master角色在多元文化软件公司中的学习路径与职业发展机制缺乏系统研究。

Method: 采用两阶段研究方法：进行两项定性调查（访谈研究），并对结果进行定性和定量数据分析。

Result: 研究确定了SM需求与团队规模相关，六名成员是上限；不存在统一的软技能学习流程或进展衡量标准；部分公司根据客户反馈或团队反馈衡量软技能，另一些公司则同时考虑两者；许多SM软技能学习举措以员工自发行动为基础。

Conclusion: Scrum Master在多元文化软件公司中的学习路径具有高度非结构化特征，软技能发展主要依赖员工主动性，公司层面缺乏标准化的学习与评估体系，这为敏捷角色人才培养领域提供了重要实证依据。

Abstract: Context: Agile development methodology has been widely adopted by industry and the demand for experienced professionals in Agile-related roles is persistently high. Objectives: We focus on the learning path for a Scrum Master role in multicultural software companies and investigate the role in relation to team size, together with the learning process for a career path, and how companies monitor soft skills development. Method: We conducted our study in two phases, two qualitative surveys (interview studies) and performed a qualitative and quantitative data analysis of the results. Conclusions: Our results identified that the need for a Scrum Master (SM) depends on the size of the team, with our study indicating a six-member limit. There is no overall standardized process for soft skills learning or metrics to measure progress. Some companies measure soft skills based on feedback received from the client or from the team, and other companies are taking both types of feedback into consideration. Many learning initiatives, especially on soft skills for an SM role, were based on actions of the employees.
  The version of Record of this contribution is published in Software Engineering and Advanced Applications. SEAA 2025. Lecture Notes in Computer Science, vol 16083. Springer, Cham. and available online at: check DOI.

</details>


### [40] [T(r)opical Islands: Visualizing & Understanding Socio-Technical Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05254)
*Adam Štěpánek,Marco Raglianti,Vít Rusňák,Jan Byška,Barbora Kozlíková,Michele Lanza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种3D可视化方法，将GitHub等协作开发平台上项目的社会技术工件（如bug报告、功能请求、论坛帖子）中的主题映射为植被覆盖的岛屿，工件被表示为各种类型的树木，通过岛屿的起伏展示主题讨论热度随时间的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 项目中的非代码工件分散在多个通信渠道中且以自然语言书写，碎片化且噪声大，使得分析项目演变变得困难，现有方法难以有效地分析和理解这些社会技术工件及其演化。

Method: 构建了从数据挖掘到交互式渲染的完整可视化流水线原型，利用机器学习技术对工件进行聚类和主题提取，将主题映射到三维植被覆盖的岛屿上，个体工件表示为不同类型树木，岛屿随主题讨论热度上升而露出海面、降温时下沉。

Result: 通过多个案例研究展示了该方法能揭示项目历史中开发主题讨论的洞察；用户研究（N=34）验证了该方法用于理解社会技术工件及其演化的适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的3D主题岛屿可视化方法能够有效帮助用户理解项目中碎片化的社会技术工件及其随时间的演化过程，用户研究结果支持了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Projects hosted on collaborative software development platforms, such as GitHub, include many non-code artifacts documenting the project's lifecycle, with its challenges, plans, design, and even community. These socio-technical artifacts include, for example, bug reports, feature requests, and forum posts, offering a useful prospect on the project's evolution. However, these artifacts are dispersed over multiple communication channels and written in natural language, making their analysis difficult, as they are fragmented and with considerable noise.
  We present a 3D visualization approach mapping topics found across a project's socio-technical artifacts onto vegetation-covered islands, where the individual artifacts are depicted as trees of various types. The topic islands rise out of the ocean as they become discussed, to sink again when they are no longer so. We built a prototype implementing the entire visualization pipeline, from data mining to interactive rendering, leveraging machine learning techniques to cluster the artifacts and extract their topics. We present, through several case studies, the insights that our approach elicits about discussions of development topics throughout a project's history. The user study we conducted (N=34) further strengthens our conclusions about its suitability for understanding socio-technical artifacts and their evolution.

</details>


### [41] [Ritgard: T(r)opical Islands of Socio-Technical Artifacts on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05278)
*Adam Štěpánek,Marco Raglianti,Jan Byška,Barbora Kozlíková,Michele Lanza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Ritgard，一个将GitHub仓库中的社会技术工件（Issues、Pull Requests、Discussions）挖掘并可视化为3D岛屿的工具。每个岛屿代表一个主题，岛屿上的树木代表单个工件，岛屿的地形随主题的活跃与衰落而升降，从而展现项目特性与关注点的演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件项目中存在大量非代码工件（社会技术工件，STAs），这些工件记录了源代码背后的人类过程和决策信息，如库变更的动机、架构决策、用户遇到的问题等。然而这些信息分散在不同的通信渠道中且以自然语言书写，难以被有效提取和探索。

Method: Ritgard挖掘GitHub仓库中的Issues、Pull Requests和Discussions，结合文本嵌入和文本摘要技术从工件中提取主题并将其聚类为岛屿；每个工件被可视化为岛上的树；通过岛屿地形的升降来刻画主题在项目生命周期中的活跃程度变化。

Result: 论文描述了Ritgard工具的使用方式和实现细节，并展示了工具可视化过程中面临的诸多技术挑战及其解决方案。

Conclusion: Ritgard通过3D可视化将分散的社会技术工件组织为可探索的主题图景，使得开发者能够直观理解项目架构及其演化过程，弥补了纯代码视角无法获取的信息。

Abstract: A software project is more than just code. Non-code artifacts often document the human processes and decisions behind source code. The rationale behind a library change, an architectural decision, a problem encountered by a user are all examples of information typically present in socio-technical artifacts (STAs), created and persisted in channels separate from the repository itself (yet sometimes very close---e.g., GitHub Issues with GitHub repositories). These STAs are a trove of information about the project's architecture and its evolution, containing details and insights that code alone cannot provide. Unfortunately, this information is not easily extracted and explored as STAs are frequently fragmented over different communication channels, and are written in natural language.
  We present Ritgard, a tool that mines GitHub repositories for their STAs, namely Issues, Pull Requests, and Discussions, and visualizes them as 3D islands covered with trees. Each tree represents a single artifact and each island is a topic extracted from the artifacts through a combination of text embedding and text summarization. The terrain of the islands rises out of the ocean as the topic becomes active and sinks back in when it becomes stale, thus depicting the evolution of features and concerns throughout the project's lifetime. We describe the tool's usage and implementation, showing the numerous technical challenges behind Ritgard's visualization.

</details>

### [42] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本技术报告提出EXAONE Finance，一个专为金融预测设计的时间序列基础模型（TSFM），采用无注意力架构（因果1D卷积+分组感知池化MLP）替代自注意力，通过掩码上下文增强提升对缺失数据的鲁棒性，并在涵盖股票、外汇、大宗商品、加密资产、固收和宏观指标的大规模金融语料上预训练。在FinVerse基准上，该模型在点预测精度、截面资产排序和组合盈利性三个评估层级均排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSFM主要面向通用领域时间序列，存在三大局限：1）自注意力架构的计算复杂度随序列长度和变量数呈二次增长；2）假设输入完全可观测，无法应对金融市场中普遍存在的间歇性缺失数据；3）预训练语料未能捕捉金融市场独特动态。这些问题严重制约了TSFM在金融领域的适用性，因为金融场景常涉及长序列、多通道且间歇性观测的面板数据。

Method: 1）架构创新：采用无注意力架构，以线性时间复杂度的因果1D卷积处理时间维混合，以分组感知池化MLP处理变量维混合，替代自注意力的二次复杂度计算；2）训练策略：引入掩码上下文增强（masked context augmentation），在训练过程中暴露于连续缺失片段，提升对金融数据中普遍缺失的鲁棒性；3）预训练数据：在大规模金融语料上预训练，覆盖股票、外汇、大宗商品、加密资产、固定收益及宏观经济指标等多类资产。

Result: 在FinVerse金融预测基准（覆盖多样化资产类别）上，EXAONE Finance在全部三个评估层级——点预测精度、截面资产排序和组合盈利性——均取得最优性能，达到最先进水平（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 通过无注意力线性复杂度架构与针对金融数据特性的预训练设计（多资产语料+掩码增强），EXAONE Finance验证了专门面向金融领域定制TSFM的有效性，为长序列、多通道、间歇观测的金融时间序列建模提供了可行方案，并全面超越现有通用TSFM在该领域的表现。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [43] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对人工智能招聘系统的发展历程进行了系统化叙述性综述，追踪了从双边检索与排序、神经人岗匹配、大语言模型组件到工具型招聘代理的演化，分析了相似性到互惠适配、单一模型到复合工作流、离线预测到证据与产出对齐评估三大耦合转变，并提出了从评估证据到可辩护结论的分级映射及研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘中的人工智能自动化对象已从简档案对和排名列表转向多阶段工作流，涉及证据检索、候选人比较以及行动支持或执行。然而现有评估框架未能跟上这一转变，行为标签混淆了曝光、偏好与资质，私有和合成数据限制了外部效度，最终输出分数掩盖了流水线内部失败，且隐私未被直接评估、没有单行工作联合评估效用、公平性、隐私与安全性，亟需系统性梳理与新的评估视角。

Method: 采用有目的的检索与编码协议（更新至2026年7月23日，并附带至2026年9月2日的定向更新），纳入40篇代表性工作及配套的工业与法律来源。该综述并非流行率估计，而是对代表性工作的定性综合分析；引入领域级、配对级、列表级、案例级、轨迹级和结果级的证据水平区分，并提出从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分级映射。

Result: 梳理出三大耦合转变（相似性→互惠适配、单模型→复合工作流、离线预测→证据与产出对齐评估），识别出招聘AI在文档理解、检索、排序、评估、面试、寻源与人机交接等环节中的主要证据缺口——特别是行为人标签的混淆问题、私有与合成数据的局限、最终输出分数掩盖管线失败，以及效度、公平性、隐私、安全四者无法在同一样本中联合评估。

Conclusion: 提出以互惠、证据扎根、时间受控、选择性、可审计为导向的系统化研究议程。系统进展的评判标准应是：工作流是否能检索到正确的证据、是否保留不确定性、是否支持可争议的决策，以及在明确的成本与风险约束下是否改善招聘产出。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>

Method: （1）开发基准适配器（adapters），将80多个基准标准化以评估任意智能体，并通过严格代码审查和一致性实验（parity experiments）验证适配正确性；（2）开展大规模评估，使用Terminus-2及3种原生测试框架（harnesses）对8个不同能力层次的模型在54个基准上进行测试；（3）通过难度过滤、AI与人工审计、以及"审计-修复"循环，从适配套件中提炼出Harbor-Index——包含来自29个基准的82个高难度、多样化、高质量任务。

Result: Harbor-Index在保持大规模智能体评估的挑战性和广度的同时成本可控。所有被评估的"模型-测试框架"配置通过率均不超过30%，最强配置（GPT-5.5 with Codex）达到28.0%的通过率，表明这些任务确实具有高难度和区分度。

Conclusion: 开放源码发布了适配器、评估结果、深度分析以及Harbor-Index，旨在为语言模型智能体的评估提供更可靠、更全面的基础设施支持。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究利用机器学习算法对电力系统事故（contingency）的安全等级进行分类（安全、中等、严重）。采用Newton-Raphson潮流法提取事故场景数据，以总体性能指数（OPI）为安全度量；使用SMOTE处理类别不平衡、PCA进行降维，在IEEE-14和IEEE-30母线系统上基于N-k（k=1,2,3）事故场景生成数据集，评估KNN、随机森林和SVM三种分类器在四种混合预处理配置下的性能。随机森林表现最佳（IEEE-30 F1=0.97，IEEE-14 F1=0.86），实验表明PCA对整体性能贡献大于SMOTE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保电力系统安全对供电稳定性和可靠性至关重要，有效的事故分类能够支持主动决策并降低大规模故障风险。传统事故分析方法的实时性不足，因此需要探索机器学习等可扩展的替代方案来提升安全评估的效率与准确性。

Method: 研究采用Newton-Raphson潮流法从事故场景中提取系统数据，以Overall Performance Index (OPI)作为安全分类指标。数据预处理结合SMOTE（解决类别不平衡）和PCA（降维），构建了四种混合预处理配置：归一化、SMOTE平衡、PCA变换、SMOTE+PCA联合变换。在IEEE-14和IEEE-30母线系统上通过N-k（k=1,2,3）事故场景生成数据集，训练并评估KNN、RF和SVM三种分类器，以精确率、召回率和F1分数进行性能评估，重点关注严重事故类别。

Result: 随机森林在IEEE-30系统上取得最高F1分数0.97，在IEEE-14系统上为0.86；SVM从PCA中获益显著，提升了分类准确率；KNN最适合SMOTE与PCA组合转换。实验发现PCA对模型整体性能的贡献大于SMOTE；SMOTE虽能改善召回率，但可能引入误报，降低准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究表明机器学习可作为传统事故分析的可扩展且高效的替代方案，特别是在PCA辅助下能够有效提升电力系统实时安全评估能力。虽然SMOTE有助于改善召回率，但精确度与误报之间存在权衡，实际应用中需根据安全需求合理选择预处理策略。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [46] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两个搜索智能体Iris-mini（35B-A3B）和Iris-pro（397B-A17B），以及其背后的数据管道和训练流程。通过反向构造多跳推理问题生成训练数据，先经SFT后经RL优化，采用称为"SFT-RL攀升"的两阶段交替程序。在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE基准上分别达到82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3和88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4的成绩，是各自参数范围内最强的开源搜索智能体结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放源代码搜索智能体领域的模型在推理能力和训练方法上仍缺乏系统性的数据管道与训练配方。作者旨在解决两个关键问题：一是如何高效构造高质量的多跳搜索训练数据（包括问题与轨迹）；二是如何在SFT与RL之间建立有效的迭代循环，使模型不断在更难的搜索任务上取得进展。此外，作者还强调推理时上下文管理（inference-time context management）对搜索智能体的影响往往被低估，需要在受控条件下进行公平评估。

Method: 数据构建采用"反向构造"策略：从网页语料的超链接结构中提炼实体图，从种子页面及其外链构建多跳链；将非答案实体改写为描述性引用，杜绝字符串匹配的捷径；仅保留参考模型闭卷失败但提供证据后可解的问题。随后将问题转化为轨迹，在轨迹级和回合级双重过滤后用于SFT。RL阶段面向实时搜索进行优化，奖励评判器和观测摘要器部署在训练集群内部，过长的回滚在请求级别中断并从已提交的前缀处恢复。SFT与RL交替进行（称为SFT-RL攀升），每个RL轮次中最难被解决且效率最高的回滚被返送到下一轮监督训练。评估时在保持工具集、上下文限制和评判器固定的前提下，每种基准都分别以启用和不启用上下文管理两种模式评测。

Result: 在启用上下文管理的情况下，Iris-mini和Iris-pro在四个基准上的成绩分别为BrowseComp 82.2/88.6、BrowseComp-ZH 84.8/85.1、DeepSearchQA 86.9/92.9和HLE 52.3/56.4。所有结果均来自单一的ReAct智能体，无子智能体和测试时验证。这些是各自参数范围内开源搜索智能体的最强整体表现。

Conclusion: 作者计划将模型权重以及完整的数据构建、训练和评估配方开源。该工作展示了数据质量过滤、SFT与RL交替迭代（SFT-RL攀升）以及推理时上下文管理对搜索智能体性能的关键作用，为该领域后续研究提供了可复现的完整框架。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [47] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种测试时的推理方法来解决LLM解释不完整（incompleteness）的问题：从输入中移除解释未提及的概念，然后重新查询模型。该方法无需修改模型参数，可同时保留提及概念的影响并消除未提及的隐藏影响，在多个数据集和模型族上提升了解释的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于关键决策，其解释是审计模型行为的重要工具，但解释常常不忠实：不完整（遗漏影响答案的因素）和不可靠/不健全（引用未影响答案的因素）。现有的改进方法中，训练时方法需要访问模型权重和大量计算资源，而测试时方法主要聚焦于解决不健全问题，缺乏直接针对不完整问题的测试时方案。

Method: 提出一种测试时的推理方法：从输入中移除解释中未提及的概念（即未被解释归因的影响因素），然后用缩减后的输入重新查询模型。这样做的效果是：消除了解释中未提及的潜在影响（即不完整性的来源），同时保留了那些被解释提及的概念对答案的影响。该方法与模型无关，不需要修改模型参数，可灵活应用于推理阶段。

Result: 在两个数据集、多个模型家族以及两种独立的忠实度评估指标上，该方法相比标准提示方法和鼓励忠实的提示方法均显著提升了解释的忠实度。

Conclusion: 所提出的测试时方法能够有效减少隐藏影响（即解释中未提及但实际影响模型输出的因素），改善LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性，为审计和提升LLM解释忠实度提供了一种灵活的机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [48] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现，LLM个体能力的提升反而可能降低系统级表现。由于共享训练数据和架构，更强的LLM行为会更趋同，产生无法分散的相关性风险。这种"能力悖论"通过金融市场中的多智能体模拟得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被大规模部署在金融、内容审核、招聘等具有重大影响的真实系统中，但现有研究主要关注个体模型能力提升，忽视了系统层面由多智能体共同决策可能产生的结构性风险。作者假设共享训练数据和架构会使能力更强的模型行为更相似，从而产生无法通过多元化消除的相关性风险。

Method: 作者提出了一个通用框架，解释模型间相关性如何产生不可分散的风险下限。随后在金融市场中构建了基于智能体的模拟系统，使用不同通用能力的LLM作为交易者，检验框架的理论预测。

Result: 研究有三个主要发现：(1) 前沿LLM表现出显著的相关行为，且相关性随能力提升而增加；(2) 当智能体共享准确推理时，增加智能体参与可降低市场级风险；(3) 当智能体处于共同的错误信息环境中时，同样的相关行为反而成为负担。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一个"能力悖论"：提升个体模型能力不一定产生更好的系统级结果。这一动态是否在其他领域同样出现，是需要进一步验证的开放实证问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [49] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业语言模型（CLM），一个将企业显性与隐性知识转化为本体锚定的企业级基础架构框架，通过五个能力平面与四大架构支柱实现可推理、可执行的治理式企业AI部署，并在一家巴西三级医院中完成三个成熟度阶段的实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败的根源并不在于模型能力不足，而在于组织缺乏结构化的载体来编码其决策、协商与执行逻辑。通用大语言模型（LLM）不携带企业特定的本体先验；RAG检索增强生成方法难以支撑可执行行动；静态剧本虽编码了逻辑却无法推理与适应。因此需要一种从设计之初就将隐性知识捕获、本体锚定、主权部署与可审计执行协同设计的架构。

Method: CLM框架由五个能力平面和四大架构支柱构成。五大能力平面分别承载结构化、非结构化、多模态与隐性知识；四大支柱包括：(1) Neurosymbolic Mesh——将生成模型与知识图谱耦合成神经符号混合架构；(2) Skill Graph——将可复用的战术、角色、异议与目标进行类型化组合；(3) Living Digital Twins——将职能领域建模为可推理的替代体；(4) Deep Security Layer——强化主权性、可追踪性与人类监督。此外，Spec-as-Code范式将锚定的意图桥接为可执行工件。

Result: 论文提出四项贡献：一是将CLM确立为独立研究对象；二是引入Skill Graph实现组合式可解释性（explainability by construction）；三是提出"智慧聆听者效应"（Wisdom Listener effect），即具备隐性知识能力的基础架构随使用而价值复利增长，并与动态能力及组织学习理论建立关联；四是提供来自巴西一所JCI认证三级医院在LGPD合规下的实证证据，实例化了六个成熟度阶段中的三个。

Conclusion: CLM是企业级基础架构类方案的一个具体实例，该类基础架构（foundation-centric）的核心在于将隐性知识捕获、本体锚定、主权部署与可审计执行作为一体化的先验设计而非事后补丁。未来研究可沿着成熟度阶段的完整验证与更广泛行业的落地路径展开。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [50] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioSync是一种将可穿戴设备与移动设备的多模态生理信号（心脏、神经、行为、语音）融合为连续复合数字生物标志物BSI的模型。其采用多头自注意力机制处理模态token，并加入包含标准特征拼接的线性分支，形成wide-and-deep架构。在两个合成队列实验中，BioSync在认知衰退队列中AUC达0.928，在代谢-自主神经队列中准确性/F1达0.764/0.766，略优于或与特征拼接相当；BSI与潜在疾病严重程度高度相关（r=0.91和r=0.68）。该模型在模态缺失训练下表现稳健，且优于五种已发表数字生物标志物参考值，但仍需真实队列验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴和移动设备采集的心脏、神经、行为及语音等多模态测量只能提供局部的、含噪的生理状态视图，单一模态信息有限。论文假设联合观测可能包含单一模态无法获得的信息，因此需要一个能够融合多模态信号、在BEST框架下定义连续复合数字生物标志物的统一模型，以更全面地表征生理状态。

Method: BioSync架构包含两部分：(1) multi-head self-attention层处理各模态token以捕捉模态间交互；(2) 线性分支，其假设类涵盖标准特征拼接（即wide部分）。该架构受潜在变量测量理论和联合观测信息增益的启发。模型在两个文献驱动的合成队列上评估：一个四模态认知衰退队列（HRV、EEG、actigraphy、speech），一个基于公开AI-READI可穿戴模式构建的代谢-自主神经队列。评估涵盖与拼接方法对比、消融实验（纯attention）、模态缺失训练鲁棒性测试，以及与六种预定义准则相关的单模态/早期融合/晚期融合设计的比较。

Result: 认知队列中BioSync AUC=0.928 vs 拼接AUC=0.926；代谢队列中BioSync准确性/F1=0.764/0.766 vs 拼接0.756/0.758。BSI与潜在严重程度相关性在两种队列中为r=0.91和r=0.68。纯attention消融在认知队列AUC降至0.911，证明提升来自wide-and-deep组合结构。模态缺失训练下，BioSync在六种认知队列损坏率中的五种和最高代谢损坏率下优于拼接。认知队列AUC(0.928)高于五种已发表数字生物标志物参考值，但数据集和任务差异排除受控基准声明。

Conclusion: BioSync提出的wide-and-deep多模态融合架构在合成队列中展现出略优于标准特征拼接的性能，尤其体现在鲁棒性和架构消融分析上。BI结合了线性与注意力机制的优势，在模态缺失条件下表现更佳。然而，由于实验基于合成队列，必须在真实队列中进行验证才能确立其实用价值；与已有方法的比较也因数据集差异而无法构成受控基准。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [51] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench 是一个农场模拟基准测试，首次为"回避副作用"设定价格，并将其副作用命名为活体动物。通过LLM子代理驾驶拖拉机的强化学习网格世界，衡量模型愿意付出多少代价来避免伤害动物，而非仅仅口头表态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的副作用基准测试仅衡量模型是否造成副作用，但没有量化模型为避免副作用愿意付出的代价；且从未将副作用的对象明确命名为活体生物，以测试模型对"生命"与"普通物体"的道德区分。

Method: 构建农场模拟网格世界：LLM子代理驾驶两台无记忆拖拉机进行合作玉米收获。当动物阻挡路线时，模型需在"直接碾压（无燃料成本）"和"绕行（需付燃料价格）"之间做出带价决策。设岩石（会损坏拖拉机）和干草捆（无害非生物）为两类对照组；另设"偷邻居庄稼"作为第二项道德测试。评分器直接统计游戏日志事件计数，不使用LLM评判器，完全可复现。

Result: 在9个模型、7201次定价决策中，3951次涉及动物。击杀率从0.4%到98.8%不等（Terra和Sol最仁慈，GPT-4o-mini最残忍），且与模型能力无关。9个模型中的6个对价格敏感（5%显著水平），弹性系数0.09-1.69。所有模型在默认地图上碾压野生动物多于农场动物。道德简报的影响最大：在道德简报下，6个推理模型中有5个击杀率低于6%；移除简报后，6个模型击杀率均超过84%。

Conclusion: HarvestBench揭示了不同大语言模型在"为回避伤害愿意付出多少代价"这一维度上存在巨大且不与能力排序的差异；模型行为强烈受道德提示（briefing）影响，说明其对生命的保护倾向是上下文驱动的而非内化的稳定偏好。该基准完全基于游戏日志的可复现性强，衡量的是真实行为而非口头表述。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [52] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出APT-RAG框架，通过自适应规划和拓扑感知的证据收集来解决证据密集型多跳问答中结构化RAG方法的局限性，在基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化RAG方法在证据密集型QA中面临两大关键局限：一是结构刚性，限制了推理结构的自适应扩展；二是忽视拓扑结构的证据收集，无法有效整合分布在数十甚至数百个文档中的证据。

Method: APT-RAG包含三个核心组件：(1)自适应规划(Adaptive Planning)，根据问题依赖性和证据需求动态扩展推理结构；(2)拓扑感知证据收集(Topology-aware Evidence Gathering)，通过兄弟证据复用、直接检索和子节点证据聚合来提高证据覆盖率；(3)证据引导的批量答案生成(Evidence-guided Batched Answer Generation)，以减少证据密集型QA中的生成开销。

Result: 在证据密集型QA基准测试上，APT-RAG优于现有的结构化RAG方法。

Conclusion: APT-RAG通过动态规划结构与拓扑感知的证据整合，有效解决了证据密集型多跳问答中的结构刚性和证据分散问题，展现了结构化RAG在复杂证据整合任务中的潜力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [53] [PerfReasoning: How Well Do LLMs Reason on Hardware Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04476)
*Dan Zhao,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam,Christos Kozyrakis,Qijing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PerfReasoning基准测试，评估LLM在硬件性能建模中的能力，涵盖两种任务：(1)直接推理以比较映射方案、(2)生成分析性能模型代码。结果显示最强闭源模型在推理问答上超90%，开源最佳达82.4%，但模型代码构建难度远高于推理——除GPT-5.6Sol外其余模型平均通过率低于15%且运行间波动大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能建模对硬件设计和软件优化至关重要，但构建这类模型需要对计算、数据复用、存储和移动进行结构化推理。现有研究缺乏系统评估LLM在此领域能力的基准，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建PerfReasoning基准，在给定工作负载、架构和映射规格的条件下，让模型执行两类任务：比较映射方案（推理式Q&A）和预测片外流量与缓冲区需求（生成分析性性能模型代码）。同时探索任务特定强化学习（RL）和无反馈多轮自修订提示对提升模型性能的效果。

Result: 最强闭源模型在基于推理的Q&A上超90%，最佳开源模型达82.4%；但模型构建任务显著更难——除GPT-5.6 Sol超80%通过率外，其他所有模型配置平均低于15%且跨运行变化显著。任务特定RL将4B模型的映射推理准确率提升15.7个百分点，而无反馈的多轮自修订提示不够可靠。

Conclusion: PerfReasoning揭示了LLM在表面合理的架构推理与可靠的性能模型构建之间存在明显差距。作者将公开该基准以支持可复现评估并追踪未来进展。

Abstract: Performance modeling is central to hardware design and software optimization, yet constructing these models requires structured reasoning about computation, data reuse, storage, and movement. We introduce PerfReasoning, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs both as direct performance reasoners and as generators of analytical performance-model code. Given workload, architecture, and mapping specifications, models compare mappings and predict off-chip traffic and buffer requirements. The strongest closed-source models exceed 90% on reasoning-based Q&A, and the best open-weight model reaches 82.4%. However, model construction is substantially harder: while GPT-5.6 Sol exceeds 80% pass rate, all other model configurations average below 15% and vary markedly across runs. Task-specific RL raises a 4B model's mapping-reasoning accuracy by 15.7 points, whereas feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting is not reliably effective. PerfReasoning exposes the gap between plausible architectural reasoning and reliable performance-model construction. We will publicly release the benchmark to support reproducible evaluation and track future progress.

</details>


### [54] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM升级时不同记忆存储方式的迁移可靠性，发现固定模式知识图谱（KG-fixed）迁移最稳定，压缩笔记（NOTES）对模型高度耦合且迁移后精度波动大，RAG系统部分嵌入迁移仅能获得部分增益，保留原始历史对记忆修复至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型升级是常规操作，但记忆迁移（memory migration）这一核心问题常被忽视。当LLM升级时，即使保留相同的记忆存储，新模型可能无法正确解读旧记录，混合嵌入版本可能破坏检索，且原始证据缺失时修复也无法进行。作者旨在系统比较不同记忆表示形式在模型升级场景下的迁移可靠性和修复可行性。

Method: 作者使用48条合成历史记录（含随机化答案代码），采用精确评分方法，在两种子100亿参数的开源权重模型之间进行方向性与逆向（双向）的writer swap实验。比较四种记忆表示：LC-RAW（原始全文写入长上下文）、RAG（分块+检索增强生成）、NOTES（模型压缩为自然语言笔记）和KG-fixed（固定模式知识图谱）。测试内容包括完整迁移、50/50混合嵌入的部分迁移、诊断性分解以及store-only修复实验。

Result: KG-fixed迁移最可靠，精度仅变化+0.0004±0.0020；NOTES高度模型耦合，精度不对称地变化+9.91或-13.28个百分点（取决于迁移方向）；RAG的50/50混合索引部分迁移仅获得4.96点增益，远低于完整重嵌入的11.90点。诊断分解显示NOTES精度缺陷的80%源于构建初期信息丢失，RAG缺陷的81%源于检索失败。store-only修复中，NOTES在所有48个测试案例均无法达到90%恢复目标，而保留原始历史在48例中有34例可成功恢复。

Conclusion: 固定模式结构（如KG）在模型升级中具有最佳迁移稳定性；压缩表示高度依赖生成它的模型，需要考虑方向性迁移测试；RAG系统必须保持严格的嵌入空间隔离而非部分迁移；保留原始源历史是记忆修复成功的关键前提。

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [55] [When Quantization Breaks Memory: Recurrent-State Write-Back in Low-Precision Temporal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04490)
*Ismail Erbas,Xavier Intes,Vikas Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"循环状态写回"（recurrent-state write-back）概念，研究低精度量化下循环神经网络中状态存储规则对推理的影响。在荧光寿命成像的紧凑GRU编码器-解码器中，使用确定性4位状态存储替代连续状态传播会使τ1和τ2估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍。误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆可在不重训练的情况下恢复精度，且该现象在LSTM中同样复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化是降低神经网络推理计算和内存需求的主流手段，但循环网络与传统前馈网络不同：量化后的状态会被存储并在下一时间步返回，因此状态存储规则本身会影响后续计算轨迹。然而此前研究未将这一环节独立出来系统考察，缺乏对"状态写回规则"这一关键设计变量的认识。

Method: 作者：(1) 定义"循环状态写回"概念并将其效应在紧凑GRU编码器-解码器中隔离分析；(2) 在保持训练好的模型不变的前提下，将连续状态传播替换为确定性4位状态存储并对比误差；(3) 引入误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆三种策略传递被抑制更新的信息，无需重训练即可恢复精度；(4) 进行精度扫描和匹配训练实验分别验证精度提高的负面效应以及状态接口兼容性的可学习性；(5) 在独立训练的LSTM中重复后训练干预，验证跨架构泛化性，并分别干预cell state和hidden state以比较其敏感性。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 固定训练模型的条件下，4位确定性状态存储使τ1和τ2的估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍；(2) 失败根源在于重复的小幅更新低于写入阈值，导致存储状态几乎不变而网络仍在提议变更；(3) 误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆可跨时间传递被抑制更新的信息并在无重训练条件下恢复精度；(4) 状态精度单调提高并不必然改善效果，可能恶化一个固定的循环解；(5) 匹配训练表明状态接口的兼容性可以习得；(6) 在LSTM中同样复现该失败模式，且cell state比hidden state对粗粒度写回更敏感。

Conclusion: 循环状态写回是决定低精度循环动力学行为的关键因素，状态存储接口应被视为量化循环推理的核心设计考量。该发现对任何涉及循环状态量化的推理场景（如边缘部署、超低比特硬件加速等）具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the computational and memory demands of neural-network inference. In recurrent networks, however, the quantized state is stored and returned at the next time step, so the rule used to store that state can alter subsequent computations. Here, we introduce recurrent-state write-back to denote this rule and isolate its effect in a compact GRU encoder--decoder for fluorescence lifetime imaging, a molecular imaging modality used in quantitative biological imaging. A central task is estimating two lifetime parameters, the short-lived component τ1 and the long-lived component τ2, from high-noise time-resolved fluorescence signals. Holding the trained model fixed, replacing continuous state propagation with deterministic 4-bit state storage increases estimation errors for τ1 and τ2 by approximately 70x and 300x, respectively. Failure occurs when repeated small updates remain below the write threshold, leaving the stored state nearly fixed while the network continues to propose change. Error feedback, residual memory, and direction memory carry information from these suppressed updates across time and recover accuracy without retraining. Precision sweeps show that increasing state precision can worsen a fixed recurrent solution, while matched training shows that compatibility with the state interface can be learned. To test whether this behavior extends beyond the GRU, we repeat the post-training intervention in an independently trained LSTM, where coarse write-back reproduces the failure, error feedback restores accuracy, and state-specific interventions reveal greater sensitivity of the cell state than the hidden state. Our results establish recurrent-state write-back as a key determinant of low-precision recurrent dynamics and identify the state-storage interface as a central design consideration for quantized recurrent inference.

</details>


### [56] [ResLearn-XR: Residual Learning for Network Traffic and Quality-of-Experience-Aware Modeling in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04493)
*Yoga Suhas Kuruba Manjunath,Jie Gao,Lian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ResLearn-XR框架，采用两阶段残差学习的时序结构预测扩展现实（XR）网络流量并估计体验质量（QoE）风险，在流量预测与QoE风险估计上分别较单阶段基线降低SMAPE最高达17.84%和87.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR网络流量具有突发性强、非平稳的动态特性，传统单阶段模型难以同时准确预测流量与估计QoE风险，因此需要一种能够适应XR流量动态变化并实现端到端QoE风险估计的框架。

Method: ResLearn-XR采用两阶段时序学习结构：基础序列预测模型辅以任务特定的残差学习组件。流量预测的残差学习在值空间（value space）操作，而QoE风险估计的残差学习在logit空间操作。QoE风险分支引入Data Descriptor Algorithm（DDA），一种因果特征构建模块，将数据包级应用层可观测量转换为帧时序感知的描述符，适用于加密流量分析。同时构建了XR Traffic-QoE数据集，将连续XR流量轨迹与会话级用户报告的QoE标签配对。

Result: ResLearn-XR在帧计数、帧大小和到达间隔时间预测上SMAPE最高降低17.84%，在QoE风险估计上SMAPE较单阶段基线最高降低87.8%。

Conclusion: 两阶段残差学习框架能有效提升对突发性、非平稳XR网络流量的预测精度以及QoE风险的估计准确度，证明了值空间与logit空间分离式残差学习的有效性，且DDA模块使加密流量场景下的QoE估计成为可能。

Abstract: We present ResLearn-XR, a residual learning framework for predicting eXtended Reality (XR) network traffic and estimating Quality-of-Experience (QoE) risk. ResLearn-XR adopts a two-stage temporal learning structure comprising a base sequence prediction model augmented with task-specific residual learning components to improve adaptability to bursty, non-stationary XR traffic dynamics. The residual learning stages operate in the value space for continuous XR traffic forecasting and in the logit space for probabilistic QoE risk estimation. \rev{For the QoE-risk branch, we introduce a Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA), a causal feature-construction module that converts packet-level application-layer observables into frame-timing-aware descriptors suitable for encrypted traffic analysis. We also construct an XR Traffic-QoE dataset that pairs continuous XR traffic traces with session-level user-reported QoE labels. ResLearn-XR reduces SMAPE by up to 17.84% across frame-count, frame-size, and inter-arrival-time prediction, while reducing QoE-risk estimation SMAPE by up to 87.8% over single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [57] [Rethinking Indirect Prompt Injection as a Test-Time Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04495)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Joon Sik Kim,Blazej Manczak,Vaikkunth Mugunthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了间接提示注入的搜索形式化框架，引入具有专用搜索工具的代理攻击者，通过环境侦察、结构化策略推理和基于受害者反馈的自适应评估来发现和利用漏洞。研究发现攻击者测试时计算量的增加能提升漏洞发现和利用效果，且明确的策略管理对避免冗余搜索和维持收益至关重要，提示安全评估应同时刻画攻击者的搜索过程和计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对间接提示注入攻击的安全评估通常将攻击成功视为受害者模型的固有属性，忽略了攻击者自身的搜索过程和计算资源对攻击成败的影响。作者希望识别攻击者的自适应搜索作为工具型代理系统未充分探索的安全风险。

Method: 将间接提示注入形式化为测试时对任务相关攻击表面的搜索，该攻击表面由环境、用户任务和注入任务共同诱导。具体实现了代理攻击者，包含专用搜索工具，执行环境侦察、对攻击策略的结构化推理，以及利用受害者代理反馈进行的自适应评估。通过异质任务上的实验和消融研究来验证。

Result: 在多个异质任务上，增加攻击者的测试时计算量能够提升漏洞发现和利用效果。消融实验表明显式的策略管理对于避免冗余搜索以及在大计算预算下维持攻击收益至关重要。

Conclusion: 代理安全评估应同时刻画攻击者的搜索过程和计算预算，而非将攻击成功视为独立于预算的受害者属性。攻击者对系统攻击表面的自适应搜索是工具型代理面临的重要且未充分探索的安全风险。

Abstract: We formulate indirect prompt injection as a test-time search over a task-dependent attack surface induced by the environment, user task, and injection task. To operationalize this formulation, we introduce an agentic attacker with a dedicated search harness that performs environment reconnaissance, structured reasoning over attack strategies, and adaptive evaluation using victim-agent feedback. Across heterogeneous tasks, we find that increasing attacker test-time compute improves vulnerability discovery and exploitation, while ablations show that explicit strategy management is important for avoiding redundant search and sustaining gains at larger budgets. These results suggest that agentic security evaluations should characterize both the attacker's search procedure and compute budget, rather than treating attack success as a budget-independent property of the victim. More broadly, our findings identify the attacker's adaptive search over the system attack surfaces as an important and underexplored security risk for tool-using agents.

</details>

*Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Qijia He,Runhao Li,Yinghao Li,Xupeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过仓库级编码任务中的大规模消融实验，隔离了多harness强化学习配方中的两个选择之一——组内相对优势的分组规则，发现评估harness而非训练分组规则是决定性能的主导变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习（Agent RL）越来越多地在完整执行环境中运行，多harness配方混合了两种选择：向策略暴露多个harness，以及在单一相对优势组内比较它们的奖励。作者希望隔离第二种选择，探究"跨harness信用分配是否比harness内信用分配带来更强的可迁移能力"，以厘清多harness训练中哪些设计选择真正影响性能。

Method: 从同一个Qwen3-8B监督热启动出发，作者回放了来自Aider、OpenHands、Qwen Code和SWE-agent的相同冻结任务-harness记录，使用相同数量的更新，在两种组相对策略优化（GRPO）规则下训练：Within（每个任务-harness对一组）和Cross（任务内合并harness）。在4个源harness和1个训练中保留的最小harness上，用密封的SWE-bench Verified oracle对所有检查点打分，共进行了24,000次密封评估。此外还进行了留一法分类器分析以及部分训练数据on-policy重收集的补充实验。

Result: 评估harness是主导变量：在24,000次密封评估中，它将平均解决率从2.14%移至9.27%，相差4.3倍，而训练配方仅移动1.16。分组规则并非关键因素：在保留harness上，Cross减Within为+0.25个百分点（95%置信区间[-0.48,+1.02]，8次尝试/任务），3个训练种子的合并结果为+0.16[-0.41,+0.72]，且个体估计的符号发生变化。每种规则自身的种子范围（0.42-0.45 pp）超过了规则间的差异。两种规则的最大增益落在同一源harness上。池化的优势携带着harness信息：留一法分类器从Cross的优势（+4.48 pp）中恢复了生成harness，而Withh完全没有；两种规则在保留harness上仍然达到相同的得分和动作分布。部分on-policy重新收集训练数据也没有改变这一结论。

Conclusion: 跨harness信用产生的是配置适配，而不是比harness内信用更便携的能力。多harness RL报告应明确分组边界，并在未见过的harness上测试。

Abstract: Agent reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly runs through full execution harnesses, and a multi-harness recipe mixes two choices: exposing the policy to several harnesses, and comparing their rewards inside one relative-advantage group. We isolate the second choice in repository-level coding. From one Qwen3-8B supervised warm start we replay the same frozen task-harness records from Aider, OpenHands, Qwen Code, and SWE-agent, with the same number of updates, under two rules for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), Within (one group per task-harness pair) and Cross (harnesses pooled within a task), and score every checkpoint with a sealed SWE-bench Verified oracle on four source harnesses and a minimal harness held out of training. The evaluation harness is the dominant variable: across 24,000 sealed evaluations it moves the mean solve rate from 2.14\% to 9.27\%, a factor of 4.3, where the training recipe moves it by 1.16. The grouping rule is not. On the held-out harness, Cross minus Within is +0.25 pp, 95\% confidence interval [-0.48, +1.02], at eight attempts per task, and +0.16 [-0.41, +0.72] pooled over three training seeds whose individual estimates change sign. Each rule's own seed range, 0.42 to 0.45 pp, exceeds the difference between them. Both rules place their largest gains on the same source harness. The pooled advantage carries the harness: an out-of-fold classifier recovers the generating harness from Cross's advantage +4.48 pp above the shuffled-label baseline and from Within's not at all, and the two rules still reach the same held-out score and action distribution inside each harness. Re-collecting half the training data on-policy does not change this. Cross-harness credit yields configuration adaptation and no more portable capability than within-harness credit. Multi-harness RL reports should state the grouping boundary and test under an unseen harness.

</details>

*Shangkun Wang,Nina Cai,Charles Hoong,Julian Walker,Gerson Kroiz,George Vanica,Deepak Patil,Andi Gavrilescu,Hassan Sipra,Sethu Sankaran*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为加速器编写高性能自定义内核需要深厚的硬件级专业知识，是一项复杂任务。作者希望利用大语言模型结合实时编译器反馈，构建智能体系统来自动化TPU内核的生成与优化，降低专家门槛。

Method: MaxKernel采用多智能体架构，实现三种范式：(1) HITL智能体支持协作式逐步设计；(2) Auto智能体执行全自动的基于指标/迹的优化循环；(3) 基于图的自主搜索将Auto扩展到设计空间的全局探索。三者共享一组专业子智能体池，分别负责规划、实现、自调试、测试和硬件性能剖析。

Result: 在JaxBench（包含50个多样化TPU内核任务的综合基准）以及来自开源SOTA模型的复杂真实负载上进行评估，MaxKernel一致生成高度优化的实现，匹配专家手调基线并在基准上取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: MaxKernel验证了LLM驱动多智能体系统在TPU内核自动开发中的有效性，已达到专家级水平，系统已开源。

Abstract: Designing and authoring high-performance custom kernels for accelerators is a complex task that requires deep hardware-level expertise. Large Language Models (LLM) can be leveraged together with real-time compiler feedback to build agentic systems for kernel generation. In this work, we present MaxKernel, a multi-agent system that implements three distinct paradigms for TPU kernel development: (1) a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) agent for collaborative, step-by-step design; (2) an Autonomous (Auto) agent that executes a fully automated, metric/trace-driven optimization loop; and (3) a Graph-Based Autonomous Search that scales the Auto agent for global exploration of the design space. All three paradigms leverage a shared pool of specialized sub-agents to handle planning, implementation, self-debugging, testing, and hardware profiling. We evaluate MaxKernel on JaxBench, a comprehensive suite of 50 diverse kernel tasks for TPUs, alongside complex, real-world workloads from state-of-the-art open-source models. We demonstrate that MaxKernel consistently generates highly optimized implementations, matching expert hand-tuned baselines and delivering significant performance across the benchmark. Our agent is open-sourced and available https://github.com/AI-Hypercomputer/accelerator-agents/tree/main/MaxKernel.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多材料3D打印数字材料的力学响应具有强非线性、组成依赖和速率依赖的耗散特性，而经典有限应变粘弹性模型使用闭式应变能函数和内部变量演化律，灵活性有限，难以用单一本构模型跨材料组成和加载速率进行推广。

Method: 该框架保留了经典Bergström-Boyce模型的结构（乘法运动学分解、基于不变量的应变能函数、沿归一化非平衡偏应力方向的标量耗散演化律）。平衡分支采用两种方式：直接预测闭式模型参数作为组成的函数，或利用神经常微分方程（NODEs）自动构造多凸应变能函数。非平衡分支动力学同样通过直接识别闭式参数或使用约束人工神经网络学习。采用多种组成的多速率单轴压缩数据进行训练。

Result: 在多种材料组成的多速率单轴压缩实验中，所提框架能够准确捕捉跨组成的速率依赖刚度和滞回行为，同时保持热力学一致性。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架在保留经典粘弹性模型物理结构的前提下，通过数据驱动手段实现了跨多材料组成的广义本构建模，兼具经典模型的可靠性与数据驱动方法的灵活性。

Abstract: Digital materials fabricated by multi-material 3D printing are designed as controlled mixtures of stiff and compliant constituents, yielding effective responses that span more than an order of magnitude in apparent stiffness and exhibit strongly nonlinear, composition-dependent, and rate-dependent dissipative behavior. Classical finite-strain viscoelastic models represent such behavior with closed-form strain energy functions for equilibrium and non-equilibrium stresses as well as evolution of internal variables, which may limit flexibility when a single constitutive model is expected to generalize across materials and loading rates. Here, we present a data-driven multi-material constitutive modeling framework that generalizes a formulation by Bergström and Boyce. The proposed framework retains the structure of the classical model, namely multiplicative kinematics, invariant-based strain-energy functions, and a scalar dissipative evolution law directed along the normalized nonequilibrium deviatoric stress. For the equilibrium branch, the data-driven discovery framework either directly predicts closed-form model parameters as functions of composition or automatically constructs a polyconvex strain-energy function using neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs). The nonequilibrium branch kinetics are learned similarly, either by directly identifying closed-form parameters across compositions or by using appropriately constrained artificial neural networks. Using multi-rate uniaxial compression data across multiple material compositions, we show that the proposed formulation captures rate-dependent stiffness and hysteresis across compositions while preserving thermodynamic consistency.

</details>


### [62] [From Answers to Interpretations: Rethinking Ambiguity-Induced Aleatoric Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04543)
*Omer Nahum,Niv Nayman,Jonathan Fhima,Alon Zolfi,Jeremy Levy,Shai Mazor,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种"仅澄清"（clarification-only）的方法来直接估计由输入歧义引起的不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty），无需生成答案，从而在降低计算成本的同时提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）部署中的一个关键挑战是区分不确定性来源：任务本身固有的可变性（aleatoric uncertainty）与模型知识不足带来的不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）。现有分解方法通过生成多个澄清、在每种澄清下查询答案并比较答案来估计aleatoric uncertainty，但作者认为答案并非识别歧义的必要条件——它们通常是冗余的、增加了不必要的成本，并且可能通过"认知泄漏"（epistemic leakage）引入误导性信息。

Method: 作者从理论上论证了为了识别歧义，生成答案是不必要的。据此提出了一种仅生成澄清的（clarification-only）方法，直接从输入的合理解释空间（interpretation space）中估计由歧义引起的不确定性成分，而不需要对澄清后的输入生成答案。

Result: 在三个基准上使用歧义检测作为操作性评估指标，该方法将AUROC从60.85提升至63.34；输出令牌的计算成本降低4-26倍，API调用成本降低2.2-3.5倍；同时得到的估计与认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）具有显著更低的相关系数。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，由歧义引起的aleatoric不确定性更适合从解释空间（interpretation space）而非响应空间（response space）进行估计，这也意味着现有依赖答案比较的分解方法可能并非最优路径。

Abstract: A key challenge in reliable LLM deployment is recognizing when uncertainty reflects irreducible variability in the task rather than limitations in the model's knowledge. In language tasks, a central source of such aleatoric uncertainty is input ambiguity or underspecification, where multiple interpretations remain plausible. Existing decomposition methods estimate aleatoric uncertainty by generating multiple clarifications of the input, querying the model for an answer under each clarification, and comparing the resulting answers. We argue that answers are not necessary for identifying ambiguity: they are often redundant, add avoidable cost, and can mislead through epistemic leakage. We support this claim theoretically, and propose a clarification-only approach that estimates this ambiguity-induced component directly from the space of plausible interpretations, without answers to the clarified inputs. Using ambiguity detection as an operational evaluation across three benchmarks, this direct approach improves AUROC (63.34 vs. 60.85), reduces computational cost by 4-26x in output tokens and 2.2-3.5x in API calls, and yields estimates with substantially lower correlation with epistemic uncertainty. Overall, our results suggest that ambiguity-induced aleatoric uncertainty is better estimated from the interpretation space than from the response space.

</details>


### [63] [IPGeoAI: Transformer-Based Geolocation with LLM Semantic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04559)
*Avinash Kadimisetty,Andy Jinqing Yu,Philip Favaloro,Wenlong Liu,Xiaolu Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IPGeoAI，一种利用Transformer编码器将IP地理定位从静态查询重构为序列建模任务的深度学习模型。通过LLM零样本特征提取将AS描述转化为结构化工件，并借助多头交叉注意力融合语义信号，在城市级地理定位上显著超越外部商业方案，离线提升6%精度并实现100%覆盖，在线生产测试中带动下游指标+0.35%提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式方法和数据库驱动方式难以解析现代网络基础设施中复杂非线性的地址分配模式，尤其在IPv6地址空间迅速膨胀及移动网络瞬态场景下表现不佳。现有技术在城市级IP地理定位精度和覆盖面上面临瓶颈，亟需一种能建模复杂分配规律、融合多源语义信息的新方法。

Method: IPGeoAI采用Transformer编码器将地理定位重构为序列建模任务，以捕获IP子网结构中的层次依赖关系。进一步通过离线预计算的Zero-Shot LLM特征提取流程，将原始嘈杂的自治系统(AS)描述转换为结构化领域元数据（如大学vs ISP、全球vs本地），并利用多头交叉注意力模块将这些语义信号融合进网络，弥合数值网络拓扑与真实世界语义身份之间的鸿沟。同时采用分层推理策略，先细化粗粒度的国家信号再推断城市级位置。

Result: 在覆盖20万个城市的专有数据集上，IPGeoAI在城市级粒度上显著优于某领先外部供应商。通过分层推理策略，在粗粒度国家信号基础上进行细化，城市级精度提升6%，覆盖范围扩展至全部流量的100%。大规模线上生产测试中，模型驱动一级下游用例指标实现了统计显著的+0.35%提升。

Conclusion: IPGeoAI验证了将深度序列建模与LLM语义特征融合相结合的范式在城市级IP地理定位中的有效性，证明跨模态信息融合可有效弥补纯数字网络拓扑在反映位置指征上的局限，为大规模在线场景下的精准定位提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate city-level IP Geolocation is an important enabler for the modern digital ecosystem, underpinning services ranging from local content delivery and targeting to digital rights enforcement. However, traditional heuristic and database-driven methods often struggle to resolve the complex, non-linear allocation patterns of modern network infrastructures, particularly within the exploding IPv6 address space and transient mobile networks. In this paper, we introduce IPGeoAI, a novel deep learning model architecture that reframes geolocation from a static lookup problem to a sequential modeling task. Our approach utilizes the Transformer Encoder to capture hierarchical dependencies inherent in IP subnet structures. We propose a method to resolve geographic ambiguity by integrating unstructured semantic context via a Zero-Shot LLM Feature Extraction pipeline. We utilize Large Language Models to transform raw, noisy Autonomous Systems (AS) descriptions into structured, domain-specific metadata (such as 'University' vs. 'ISP' or 'Global' vs. 'Local') via an offline pre-computation process. By fusing these semantic signals into the network via a Multi-Head Cross-Attention module, we bridge the gap between numerical network topology and real-world semantic identity. Extensive offline evaluation on a proprietary dataset spanning 200,000 cities demonstrates that IPGeoAI significantly outperforms a leading external vendor in city-level granularity. By adopting a hierarchical inference strategy that refines coarse-grained country signals, our model achieves a 6% improvement in city-level accuracy while extending coverage to 100% of the traffic. Furthermore, in large-scale online production tests, the model drove a statistically significant +0.35% improvement in our 1st-tier downstream use cases metric.

</details>


### [64] [Reducing Hallucinated Transcripts in Whisper via Hallucination Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04561)
*Maryam Abbasihafshejani,Murtuza Jadliwala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的推理时方法来抑制Whisper语音识别模型对无语音或极少语音输入产生的幻觉性转写，通过将解码器激活投影到与幻觉关联的低秩子空间之外来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper虽然广泛用于自动语音识别，但其生成式解码器在面对无语音或极少语音输入时会产生流畅的幻觉转写，现有方法缺乏有效的训练无关抑制手段。
